## 10. Floating VAN — functional preservation and modernisation

### 10.1 Preservation contract

Retain these proven/intended behaviours from `VanOverlayInteraction.kt`, `VanOverlaySurface.kt`, `FloatingOverlayService.kt`, `OverlayVisibilityPolicy.kt`, `OverlayRecoveryReceiver.kt`, and the Rev 3 Floating UX document: small resident assistant; single-tap compact workboard; double-tap Command Centre; long-press accessible controls; drag/snap/dock; drag-to-dismiss; minimise/restore; chat and voice; native notification service and permissions; expanded/maximised contextual workboard; trading ledger inspection; owner deep-link navigation; lifecycle persistence; no animation battery drain under occlusion; paired-device and Android OS permission gates.

### 10.2 New presentation policy

Treat Floating VAN as a sibling to the Home orb: same semantic state/event fabric but **independent tactile form**. The floating character/assistant remains recognisable and avoids obscuring third-party apps. Compact opens a tiny materialised workboard; expanded becomes a spatial assistant panel showing conversation, current mission or the chosen trading summary. Move/scroll gestures are separated. Make all hit targets and dismissal behaviour unambiguous. This is not a generic system bubble clone and not a second always-on dashboard.

### 10.3 Quick controls and direct interactions

Chat opens the correct composer and keyboard-safe anchor; voice opens a real voice session with consent/permission; Current Work routes to mission/task; Trades shows ledger source/freshness and supported inspector; Projects/Tasks/Decisions/Connected routes to canonical screens. Any approval action must enter the exact trusted biometric/owner-grant flow. Distinguish `dismiss overlay` from `cancel mission`; never map close X to external side effects.

### 10.4 Lifecycle/OS acceptance

Overlay must survive approved process restoration without resurrecting after deliberate owner dismissal. Test Android 14/15/16 restrictions, foreground service policy, microphone service type separately from overlay service type, battery optimisation consent, overlay above/below keyboard, screen-off visibility, protected Android system surfaces, split-screen, lock screen, settings revocation, on-call activity transitions, TalkBack and S24 Ultra real-device gestures. No unbounded log polling or WebView animation while hidden.

---

## 11. Continuity for every other VAN capability

### 11.1 Attention, decisions and approvals

A tactile triage list presents one prominent active item at a time with expandable context. Preserve attention source, importance, acknowledgement/snooze, historical decision identity, biometric challenge, exact action digest, owner note, expiry and stable recovery/readback. This should not become a Home state legend or large orb modal. All consequential actions remain typed and separately gated. No automatic reissue when a request times out.

### 11.2 Work/command and conversations

Preserve text/voice command entry, mission creation, acknowledgement vs completion distinction, progress/activity stream, waiting/failed/unknown states, explicit cancellation/steering/owner direction and durable history. New Work UI shows active work first, with tactile mission cards and relationship flow entry. Do not display internal task envelopes or raw JSON on the primary fold. User can access full diagnostics on demand.

### 11.3 Memory and adaptation

Preserve facts, preferences, context, history, owner corrections, provenance, misunderstanding/retraction, retention/withdrawal and learned strategies. Fact removal/withdrawal must not be confused with destructive erasure of all history or connected records. New cards can have tactile depth but historical/source truth must remain readable. Side-by-side time/provenance detail belongs in full Memory Detail, not the Home quick sheet.

### 11.4 Connected, settings and provisioning

Preserve provider readiness, consent, device identity, enrolled scopes, voice settings, notification policies, quiet hours, privacy and recovery. Existing signed provisioning supplies connection parameters; do not recreate manual host/port/token/certificate forms in the owner app. Distinguish healthy provider connection from permission to run an action. Native Android settings links maintain deep-link continuity and back stack. A polished tactile panel is allowed; decorative orb effects on sensitive settings are not.

### 11.5 Browser, ARTEMIS and remote interaction

Preserve session/lease ownership, live viewport, native device controls, task observation, escalation, download/upload consent, browser completion receipts and remote-browser security boundaries. The new UX uses uncluttered viewport-first layouts with touch-friendly toolbar and action sheets; meaningful mobile gestures must not conflict with drag-to-scroll in remote content. Never claim that a remote click succeeded absent provider readback. ARTEMIS retains test authority and device evidence, not a visually simulated emulator certification badge.

### 11.6 Automation and research

Separate workflow **candidate** from admitted version, scheduled job from live execution, and completed effect from a model's proposed action. Present library/workflow/step dependencies with the same graph pattern used in Projects, but keep per-step controls and receipts typed. Research surfaces show citations, sources, scope, read times and confidence/unknowns; web/cognitive outputs are not evidence of effects unless verified.

### 11.7 Notifications and operating-system integration

VAN Attention, Android notifications, push, Floating VAN and orb events should consume one canonical notification identity with per-channel delivery policy and dedupe. Explicit user choice determines which notifications can interrupt, appear silently or be deferred by quiet hours; urgent risk cannot be hidden just because a noncritical preview was snoozed. Any notification link resolves stable domain reference and scopes; cancelled/expired underlying items cannot still offer an executable action.


---

## 12. Engineering implementation: clean presentation, preserved functional foundation

### 12.1 Architectural boundary

```text
                    ANDROID VAN NEXT EXPERIENCE
   +---------------------------------------------------------+
   | TactileSpatialTheme (dark/light) + token/asset system   |
   | New NavGraph + screen compositions + semantic controls  |
   | Home Orb / Quick Hub / Work Flow / Trading / News       |
   | Accessible list/fallbacks + native sheets + Floating UI |
   +--------------------------+------------------------------+
                              | typed read / typed owner intent
   +--------------------------v------------------------------+
   | VanNextExperienceBridge                                 |
   | ObservedStateSource   OwnerNavigation   ApprovedIntent  |
   | AuthenticationState   DeviceCapabilities  ReceiptRead  |
   +--------------------------+------------------------------+
                              | audited admission boundary
     EXISTING VERIFIED PRODUCT FUNCTIONS (NOT VISUAL AUTHORITY)
    +----------------------+-------------------------------+
    | VAN gateway          | DIAL task/project projection  |
    | VATI read/risk/order | Hermes/Muse/Browser/ARTEMIS   |
    | device trust/consent | Voice/notifications/memory    |
    | event/session/queue  | provider freshness/evidence  |
    +----------------------+-------------------------------+
```

The clean boundary applies to **rendering and screen composition**, not to the app's identity or trusted services. Keep `com.dial.van` application ID and signing/pairing-compatible owner-data continuity unless a separately verified migration requires otherwise. Introducing a new package identifier would risk duplicating credentials, losing OS permissions, breaking existing app links and losing personal state; that is neither necessary for a visual rebuild nor authorised automatically.

### 12.2 Proposed source tree, distinct from legacy

```text
android/app/src/main/java/com/dial/van/next/
  app/              VanNextAppShell, adaptive navigation, global overlays
  design/           theme, measured tokens, icon/material families, elevation
  design/components/MaterialTile, PhysicalButton, DetailSheet, StatusCaption
  orb/              reducer, priority, animation, shader/renderer, tests
  quickhub/         event-aware compact hub + route wrappers
  home/             HomeRouteVNext + state previews
  trading/
     home/          trading hub, 5 destinations
     markets/       search/watchlist/freshness
     chart/         terminal shells, bridge, indicator/drawing controls
     positions/     positions/orders, detail, protection preview
     intelligence/  candidates, analyst, strategies, news entry
     news/          digest, detail, source/counterclaim drilldown
  work/             missions, agents, task lists, evidence controls
  flow/             project/task/agent graph, layout, timeline, list
  floating/         new surface compositions; legacy service adapter
  memory/           provenance-aware UI
  connected/        provider/device/readiness UI
  automation/       workflow library and run evidence
  browser/          mobile native browser controls and task overlays
  settings/         system/account/consent configuration
  accessibility/    semantic descriptions, focus, reduced motion
  contracts/        view-state types, adapters, route IDs, policy interfaces
  preview/          nonproduction fixtures and golden states only
```

**Import policy:** `next` screens may import `next/contracts` and explicitly admitted stable domain clients; they must not import old `command/*Screen`, `trading/ui/*Screen`, `design/VanPanel`, legacy `VanTokens` or `overlay/VanOverlayWorkboards` renderers. Bridge adapters may call existing functional controllers and models. Rev 1.1 makes this structural: the tree above is split into Gradle modules so legacy rendering is not on the new UI's classpath (§12.6). Exceptions require traceable owner-reviewed admission with a reason, expiry and replacement plan.

### 12.3 Ports, adapters and retained services

| Interface | Data admitted | No-go |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservedTradingPort` | Positions, orders, quotes/bars, source freshness, risk/eligibility and receipts | No chart execution or generated sizing |
| `ObservedWorkPort` | Projects, stages, tasks, dependency graph, agent leases/status and event revisions | No synthetic graph edges or fabricated progress |
| `AttentionPort` | Canonical event ID, severity, delivery state, owner decision references | No auto-approval from orb popup |
| `OwnerActionPort` | Exact allowed semantic action, digest, scope, owner proof, readback | No broad command access inferred from UI |
| `VoiceConversationPort` | Consent, active turn, transcript, audio/readiness and result IDs | No mic use without OS/owner permission |
| `FloatingPort` | Gesture/lifecycle service, presentation enum, hit region, state restoration | No direct dispatch from visual animation |
| `DeviceTrustPort` | Pairing state, capability policy, secure auth, installer provision | No host/token configuration spoofed in UI |
| `NewsResearchPort` | Source-grade news/event analysis, timestamps, citations and claim verification | No model-authored executable trade intent |

Adapters have versioned request/result types, explicit `Error`/`Unknown`/`Stale` states and source/correlation IDs. Avoid a global untyped map of strings or a monolithic ViewModel that permits cross-domain mutable state. Shared state is a projection, not a new state-authority layer.

### 12.4 Two-way migration for existing functionality

For each retained feature, prove `legacy feature ID → current source location and production caller → trusted provider operation → new adapter → new control → routed state → observed effect/error → regression test`. Also prove the reverse: `new tappable control → feature/authority/ownership → precise user outcome`. Anything that lacks a working source or authorised caller is a **gap candidate**, not passed simply because a screen reference exists.

Existing event handling, secure queues, pairing, overlay and trading repositories may remain physically in old packages while they are used through audited interfaces. No unnecessary big-bang rewrite of qualified backend functions. Nevertheless every admitted backend addition and improvement must be fully completed and integrated before any new frontend implementation. Retire old Compose views only after route-to-route parity tests and new visual owner approval, preserving rollback ability.

### 12.5 Inventory first; backend consolidation after F15 (historical DU-00a/DU-00b)

**Why it comes first.** The rebuild can only preserve what it can see. Measured on 15 October: 35 of the 78 surfaces cite 53 source files that exist only on unmerged PR #93, and 185 of the matrix's 428 source references point at the PR #93 head (F-027). Further owner-value work sits on PR #95 / #97 (native Muse, receipts), #92 (trading temperaments, which §7.4 uses), #70 (JEV owner surface), #72 (character mechanics) and #77 (cognitive fabric). Documented features in the `docs/` blueprints and `IMPLEMENTATION_LEDGER.md` may exist on no branch at all. If the new frontend is built against `main` alone, those functions disappear without anyone deciding to remove them.

**Current assignment:** steps 1–3 belong to PRE-001/PRE-002 read-only preparation. Steps 4–6 belong to BE-001 only after PRE-003 independent F15. Historical DU labels identify lineage, not the executable plan. All backend then finishes before any new UI.

1. **Freeze heads.** Record the exact head SHA of `main` and of every open candidate PR. Later changes to a PR re-enter through this step.
2. **Build the Feature Ledger** (`VAN_NEXT_FUNCTIONAL_PRESERVATION_LEDGER_REV1.csv`). One row per feature, from three sources: the 78-surface matrix (349 owner-action obligations), each candidate PR's feature-bearing changes, and features described in VAN blueprints and implementation ledgers. Columns: feature ID, owner goal, where it lives (branch, files, symbols, tests), backend contract, current status, disposition, owner decision reference.
3. **Classify every row:** `ON_MAIN`, `CONSOLIDATE` (on a branch; bring it to `main`), `PORT_REQUIRED` (documented or on an old build, absent from code), `REBUILD_FRONTEND_ONLY`, `SUPERSEDED` (with the superseding feature ID) or `OWNER_RETIRE`. Only the owner can mark `OWNER_RETIRE`; nothing is retired by omission.
4. **Consolidate in dependency order** through clean, owned branches with their own tests and review: PR #93's backend, gateway and VATI changes first (35 surfaces depend on them), then its Android domain and controller code, then PR #95/#97, #92 and the rest as their ledger rows require. PR #93's legacy screens are consolidated as well, unchanged: on `main` they are the **parity oracle** — the working reference that shows what each feature does — and they keep the current app usable until cutover. No blanket merge; each consolidation is reviewed on its own, and `REJECT_WITH_CAUSE` is recorded in the ledger.
5. **Generate the parity oracle** from consolidated `main`: routes and deep links, owner actions, gateway endpoints and the matrix obligations, as a machine-readable baseline (`tools/van_next/parity_baseline.json`).
6. **Gate the rebuild on it.** A checker (`tools/van_next/parity_check.py`) compares the new app's route registry, action registry and adapter bindings with the baseline. Any baseline feature without a new surface, a recorded `SUPERSEDED` link or an `OWNER_RETIRE` decision is `FEATURE_LOSS` and fails the build. The checker's failure is induced deliberately (remove one mapping, watch it fail) before it is trusted.

### 12.6 Keeping the new frontend clean (no pollution)

The new UI must look and behave like the locked design, not like the old app wearing new colours. Rules, enforced by the build rather than by review alone:

1. **Separate Gradle modules.** `:van-next:ui` (screens, components, theme) depends only on `:van-next:contracts` (view-state types, ports, route IDs) and the admitted domain modules listed in `VAN_NEXT_ADMITTED_DEPENDENCIES.json`. Legacy presentation packages (`command/*` screens, `trading/ui`, `design/VanTokens`, `design/components`, `overlay` renderers, legacy aura renderers) are **not on its classpath**, so importing them fails compilation instead of relying on a lint warning.
2. **Adapters are the only bridge.** Legacy functions reach the new UI through port adapters in `:van-next:bridge`, which may call legacy controllers and repositories but exports only `:van-next:contracts` types. Copy-pasting a legacy composable into the new module is rejected in review and caught by a duplicate-code check against the legacy UI packages.
3. **Token-only styling.** Colours, dimensions, radii, elevation and motion come from the `:van-next:ui` design tokens; a lint rule rejects literal colours and dimensions in screens.
4. **One string catalogue** built from §2.6. A lint rule rejects uncalibrated certainty wording (§2.6), "signal" and other banned trading phrases in UI strings. A positive test proves labelled market-implied likelihood and PBO text pass.
5. **No preview data in release.** Preview fixtures live in a debug-only source set. A release-build check fails if any **fixture or mock class, preview asset or fixture source set** is present, checked by compiled source-set and asset provenance, not by searching UI strings. `DEMO / NOT LIVE` is a fixture watermark. Real demo and read-only account badges ("Demo", "Read-only") are legitimate production labels. A mutation test injects mock data and must fail the build; a positive test shows a real demo account badge renders.
6. **Admission record for exceptions.** Any new dependency of `:van-next:ui` is added to the admitted-dependency file with a reason, owner, expiry and replacement plan; the file is hashed and the hash is checked in CI.
7. **Visual check.** Screenshot tests compare each new screen with the owner-approved native goldens (§2.5) in both themes; "old hierarchy in new colours" fails VA8.

### 12.7 Source-branch admission (per consolidation)

For each candidate PR, fetch the exact head, compare changed files against `main` and against the other candidates, and classify every feature-bearing change as `ADOPT_UNCHANGED`, `ADAPT`, `REBUILD_FRONTEND`, `REJECT_WITH_CAUSE` or `DEFER_UNQUALIFIED`, recorded in the Feature Ledger with commit and file provenance. A dependency tree of PRs is not an invitation to merge them together. Preserve qualifying improvements from later architecture even if older default-branch docs look simpler.

---

## 13. Data consistency, failures, caching and offline modes

### 13.1 Independent source domains

VAN displays aggregates over markets, orders/positions, DIAL tasks, Muse, Attention, voice and device state. These have different data ages and authority. A single global `LIVE` label must not hide stale positions or a disconnected task stream. Every panel carries `source_id`, `observed_at`, `revision`, optional `valid_until`, and `confidence-of-observation` distinct from probabilistic model confidence. The orb shows overall risk through deterministic precedence, while details expose each source's truth.

### 13.2 Event and snapshot reconciliation

Each source emits or polls a versioned snapshot and optionally ordered events. `event_id` deduplication is durable across process restarts within defined retention limits. Cursor gaps or source revision conflict cause a read-only fresh snapshot, not side-effect replay. For positions, order/cancel requests have stable client-generated intent identity and backend idempotency. For tasks, a newer authoritative `FAILED` event supersedes an earlier client `RUNNING` state, without a false celebratory progress animation. If source identity cannot be reconciled, render `UNKNOWN` and offer drilldown.

### 13.3 Cache policy

Cache display models and last-known quotes/positions only according to domain privacy and TTL; keep encryption for sensitive local data, provide lifecycle-aware invalidation on logout/device revocation. Cached data must show last verified update, source and age. The user can inspect historical charts, project snapshots and prior evidence offline, but cannot be told an unconfirmed action succeeded. A pending owner action awaiting reconfirmation is a distinct state from a queued readonly fetch.

### 13.4 Failure-state matrix (all major surfaces)

| State | Visible effect | Interaction policy | Acceptance proof |
|---|---|---|---|
| LOADING | Shape-matched subtle skeleton, no fabricated numbers | Local navigation works, duplicate write disabled | Simulated delay and device capture |
| EMPTY | Verified absence + next useful action | Add/view navigation if supported | Empty authoritative fixture |
| ERROR | Specific source and recovery path | Retry reads; avoid repeating uncertain writes | Failure-injection receipt |
| DEGRADED | Name missing subsystem; show what remains usable | Supported local/read-only operations remain | Partial upstream outage |
| OFFLINE | Last-known state dated; no LIVE glow | Defer/reconfirm writes | Transport loss + restoration |
| STALE | Explicit data age, source and TTL state | Restrict trade-changing affordances | Synthetic clock/TTL tests |
| UNKNOWN | Observability missing, no optimistic default | Inspect evidence or refresh | Missing field/bad version |
| ACCESS_DENIED | Real scope/paired-device explanation | Reconnect/regrant through authorised path | Permission refusal |
| CANCELLED/EXPIRED | Distinct from failed/success | No silent retry or duplicate grant | Replay/expiry tests |
| OUTCOME_UNKNOWN | Original effect outcome could not be verified | Check exact receipt first; no new effect | Induced lost response test |

### 13.5 Background and battery

Foreground Android presence does not mean polling every remote subsystem at UI refresh rates. Use visible-subscription priority, lifecycle-aware event consumers, bounded polling fallback and network event multiplexing. Tick/update rates for live quotes/DOM are source and device specific; visually expensive shaders pause offscreen. Notifications and urgent event persistence remain available independently of the Home orb animation.

---

## 14. Security, financial authority, privileges and privacy

### 14.1 Non-negotiable separation

`APP_VIEW != EXECUTION_AUTHORITY`, `CHART_ENGINE != BROKER_ADAPTER`, `LLM/JEV != RISK_AUTHORITY`, `GRAPH_DRAG != TASK_MUTATION`, `NOTIFICATION_TAP != OWNER_APPROVAL`, `CACHE != LIVE_TRUTH`, `PRESENTATION_PRIORITY != POLICY_PRIORITY`. This preserves actual VATI/owner controls even if individual implementation techniques improve after research. Any proposed modification to domain authority must be separately authorised, threat-modelled, implemented and qualified before the frontend calls it.

### 14.2 Signed owner action lifecycle

```text
owner views exact proposed effect and source evidence
             → eligibility/readiness + explicit scope
             → bound device/A4 biometric challenge when needed
             → signed one-use owner intent, exact canonical args digest
             → owning service validates policy/fence/expiry
             → effect execution by allowed domain authority
             → independent postcondition and receipt readback
             → visible VERIFIED_SUCCESS / REFUSED / FAILED / OUTCOME_UNKNOWN
```

The user may approve, reject, halt/resume, steer or request a trade action only where supported. A stylish tactile button does not expand grants. Never use LLM cognition, Muse UI, browser JavaScript, chart injection or orb state to mint owner/device authority. Source/credential material never enters shader asset, log, prompt, webview query or screenshot preview.

### 14.3 Trading order and chart boundary

The chart bridge is a bounded, untrusted display/interaction bridge. Allowed chart outputs include symbol/timeframe/drawing changes, `InspectPosition`, `DraftTradeIntent` (opens the §7.4 review sheet; carries direction, optional entry price, a *tighter* stop or a TP, never a size) and `RequestExplanation`; forbidden chart outputs include direct `placeOrder`, `modifyOrder`, `closePosition`, `cancelOrder`, raw `setLeverage`, `changeRiskLimit` and `sendBrokerCommand`. The native trading owner-intent layer handles all effectful operations with VATI and broker readback. Stop/target risk calculations remain owned by certified strategy/risk/lifecycle services, never a generated floating editor.

### 14.4 Device, privacy and recovery

Keep explicit pairing, revocation, secure storage, native biometric handling, signing and privacy mode. Sensitive absolute trading values must be masked in Home orb hub, Android notifications, floating overlay, watch/screen capture previews and position detail when policy requires it. Do not falsely guarantee screen-capture protection merely because the design includes a toggle; verify Android Secure Flag and approved sharing controls on physical device. Ensure no automated provider login or mic capture without supported owner permission. Rollback to old UI is possible without weakening release signing or downgrading sensitive stores.

---

## 15. Performance, accessibility, device behaviour and telemetry

### 15.1 Candidate targets (measured, not claimed achieved)

| Metric | Target | Instrument |
|---|---|---|
| Global screen navigation | Perceived <150 ms with cached view state | Macrobenchmark + frame times |
| Chart gesture/crosshair | Local responsiveness, 60 fps target on S24 Ultra | Tracing + device instrumentation |
| Home orb motion | Smooth within frame budget, no degraded chart frame time | Profile GPU render and JankStats |
| Cached trading first paint | <500 ms target (subject to device/cold start) | Device performance test |
| Project graph pan/zoom | Smooth focused view; lazy clusters at large size | 500-node stress test |
| Memory/thermal | No runaway Rive/WebView/graph/shader cache | Memory profiling + soak |
| Battery | Stop hidden render loops; bounded background work | Battery and frame lifecycle evidence |
| Reconnection | Honest freshness and no duplicate consequential command | Flight mode/Wi-Fi failover tests |
| News analysis | Cached summary first; report progressively loads verified sources | Tracing + synthetic provider latency |

Do not implement performance as a universal timer. Measure actual physical device and network under cold start, rotation, background, offline and memory pressure. Escalation tiers: full material effects → lower-resolution shaders → simpler specular highlights → static goldens **while preserving status, text and controls**. A premium design should degrade gracefully rather than suddenly becoming the generic flat UI that the owner rejected.

**Device scope (Rev 2 pack PT-16).** Qualification also covers:
- restoring state after process death, including pending provider and order references;
- tablet, landscape and split-screen layouts;
- dynamic type and Android-native system bars;
- battery and thermal budgets on the S24.

### 15.2 Accessibility contract

Each interactive physical module has a native semantic role/state/name, 48dp minimum target where applicable, clear focus order and explicit action. The orb has an accessible label stating the actual condition without requiring colour detection. Screen readers must reach tasks in deterministic dependency order through the alternate list view. Icon-only quick actions have names; critical approval and SL/TP values read with units and account context. Text scaling, TalkBack, switch access, contrast, large font and reduced motion are required on **both** themes. No essential information is embedded only in rasterised image text.

### 15.3 Locale, numeric precision and policy

Prices use symbol-native precision and locale-safe tabular figures, but authoritative order size/value are never calculated from rounded display numbers. Time uses observed source UTC plus chosen local presentation; distinguish market event time, article published time and analysis generated time. Forex quantities, equities, derivatives, percentages and currency conversions must be labelled accurately. Do not mix positive change in price with positive outcome for a short position.

### 15.4 Telemetry to diagnose regressions

Record non-sensitive screen transition latency, GPU/CPU/memory usage, orb frame budget, renderer fallback, graph node count/layout latency, event freshness, quote age, position refresh, news artifact age, deep-link failures, browser viewport errors, command outcome unknowns, and duplicate-event suppression. Attach source revision and artifact IDs rather than raw secrets. Observable postcondition is required for a completion claim.

## 16. Canonical requirements, dependency graph and packets

**237 requirements; 131 FULL-scope units/packets.** One generated plan replaces the separate Opus, Rev1.10 and forecasting schedules. Source IDs, source meanings and prior packet identities are preserved in crosswalks. Full mapping exists in `traceability/REQUIREMENTS_AND_DU_MAPPING.json`; no count alone establishes semantic acceptance.

### 16.1 Authority and single graph

The graph in `plan/DEVELOPMENT_PLAN.json` is the only active schedule. PRE-001 inventories source; PRE-002 completes all semantic/source/action/consumer mappings and contracts; PRE-003 independently issues F15; BE-001 consolidates admitted sources and qualifies parity; all backend units and their independent tests finish; BG-001 verifies the complete backend; UX and FE follow. Source forecast packet 003 aliases PRE-003; 033 aliases BG-001; 034 maps to FE-023; 035/036 map to final qualification/release. No second certificate or child gate can start the frontend.

### 16.2 Required producer dependencies

BE-035 qualifies execution simulation and chronology before BE-013 consumes economic evidence and before model training is admitted. BE-036 uses BE-035, existing diagnostics/scheduler and model/study registries. Forecast subunits declare concrete producer interfaces; semantic parent mappings are not blindly expanded into whole-unit dependencies. BE-032 waits for all completed capability consumers; all code-bearing units are downstream of F15 and BE-001. Every required backend and verification unit is an ancestor of BG-001.

| VAN-CX-001 | One canonical authority and backend-first completion graph: Only CANONICAL_AUTHORITY.json selects the active master and generated plan. Historical plans are immutable lineage, never runnable implementation authority. All backend and backend-verification units precede BG-001; every new UX/frontend unit depends on the same BG-001 scope. | PRE-002, PRE-003, BG-001 |
| VAN-CX-002 | Source-preserving implementation admission: Keep exact source paths, commit and content hashes, test evidence, feature obligations and ADOPT/ADAPT/REFERENCE dispositions. Retain all source candidates until their replacement or rejection is explicitly justified; never accept a reviewed module solely because its parent branch has passing CI. | BE-001, PRE-001, PRE-002 |
| VAN-CX-003 | Protection parity for immediate and pending fills: Route FILLED and PARTIAL receipts from all origins through one protection reconciler. Track REQUESTED, CONFIRMED, UNKNOWN and MISSING separately. Never register an intended local stop as proof of a venue stop. Apply the currently admitted immediate flatten/halt response unless an independently qualified owner-admitted bounded re-read/reattach policy replaces it; record timeout and unknown close outcomes. | BE-010, BE-012, BE-035 |
| VAN-CX-004 | Limit-price invariants and explicit order semantics: BUY LIMIT fills at or below its limit and SELL LIMIT at or above it, using the relevant executable quote and eligible event time. An at-touch order that is not executable within its limit rests or is refused. A capped-market order is a distinct intent/template with its own signed semantics; logging a LIMIT-to-MARKET substitution does not make it authorized. Unknown venue capabilities deny the operation. | BE-008, BE-010, BE-019, BE-035 |
| VAN-CX-005 | Pending-order expiry and event-policy propagation: Bind policy digest, order expiry, mandate validity, market-session constraints and cancel-on-event rules into the immutable execution command and pending supervisor. Effective expiry is the earliest applicable allowed deadline; absent or stale policy is not a 24-hour default. Clock and source revisions remain explicit. | BE-010, BE-014, BE-016, BE-019, BE-035 |
| VAN-CX-006 | Terminal order read models and monotonic lifecycle reduction: Project orders from the complete canonical lifecycle, ordered and deduplicated by broker event/sequence and reconciliation revision. PLACED is the code state and Working the label. Separate unfilled terminal orders from residual exposure after partial fills. Conflicting events become reconciliation-required, not last-arrival optimism. | BE-003, BE-010, BE-012, BE-032 |
| VAN-CX-007 | Executable quantisation before authority and metadata-based slippage: Use provider SymbolContract tick size, point size, digits, units, min/freeze stops and revision to build the executable preview first. Validate stop side and distance, risk in account currency and lot step on exact decimals. Risk and owner approval bind those executable values. Version relative-price versus absolute-price versus points units; recalculate and reapprove if changed. Preserve the no-widening invariant but do not hide a broker-invalid too-close stop. | BE-008, BE-009, BE-010 |
| VAN-CX-008 | Incremental ledger verification with explicit integrity envelope: Retain verified incremental anchors and periodic full checks; bind ledger identity, row sequence/count, chain root, source epoch and checkpoint lifetime. Full verification is required on first use, detected change, failed incremental proof and the admitted maximum interval. Document the distinction between immediate detection of new-row/rechain/deletion faults and delayed detection of in-place historical edits; stronger write protection or integrity monitoring may reduce that window. | BE-003, BE-009, BE-033 |
| VAN-CX-009 | Signed lockout policy activation and persistent all-origin risk: Retain 24-hour cooldown, 0.5 probation and suspension after more than two cooldowns in seven days as the accepted values, not live authority. Bind all fields, scope rules and policy revision into the signed mandate before activating a changed production policy. Keep autonomous strategy/account and owner.manual cooldown scopes as recorded by OD-38; always apply economic all-origin account drawdown, margin, exposure and protection ceilings. Persist reset events, loss history, economic peaks and day/week baselines. | BE-002, BE-006, BE-009, BE-011, BE-013 |
| VAN-CX-010 | Qualified chronological evaluator before automated strategy development: Establish one qualified evaluator version before candidate selection uses it. Separate isolation experiments from deployment-faithful evaluation; both declare code, data, controls, costs and random seeds. An independent fixture oracle checks event eligibility, bid/ask, limit/stop/expiry, partial fills, costs, cash flows and restart/risk controls. | BE-019, BE-035, BE-036 |
| VAN-CX-011 | Leakage-proof chronology and genuine walk-forward records: Persist train/validation/test intervals, strategy frozen-at time, embargo/purge rules, label horizons, warmup-only data and candidate selection cutoffs. Mutating only unavailable future data must leave prior features and decisions unchanged. A deliberately leaking implementation must fail. Yearly summaries remain descriptive, not walk-forward windows; a 2025 label does not by itself create untouched out-of-sample evidence. | BE-035, BE-036, VAN-ML-PKT-008, VAN-ML-PKT-021 |
| VAN-CX-012 | Immutable dataset, quote, calendar and cost provenance: Use a DatasetManifest with exact hashes, provider/mirror revision, permitted-use state, timezone/DST handling, bid/ask/mid semantics, bar timestamp conventions, missing/duplicate intervals and transformation code. Preserve full spread, commissions, financing, slippage, liquidity and FX assumptions per venue and period. A source access/usage gap is never hidden by calling fixtures live. | BE-008, BE-019, BE-035, VAN-ML-PKT-006 |
| VAN-CX-013 | Complete trial ledger and honest statistical interpretation: Record every tried configuration, failure, search budget, selection rule, effective trial treatment, untouched holdout and actual cost model. Label DSR and PBO according to their statistical meaning and calculation cohort, not chance of skill or trade-win probability. Economic admission is per exact dataset/evaluator/model/policy scope, separate from software completion. | BE-035, BE-036, VAN-ML-PKT-020, VAN-ML-PKT-021 |
| VAN-CX-014 | Governed weekly strategy-development lifecycle: One standing, scoped research grant may schedule offline diagnostics, generate candidate versions and evaluate them; it cannot alter live policy. Loss clusters generate hypotheses evaluated against winners, exposure/opportunity frequency and market regimes. Separate distribution drift from predictive deterioration. Preserve parents and all trials. Passing candidates appear as promotion proposals; failures and system faults stay inspectable. Live promotion uses a fresh exact artifact/policy-bound owner decision and stage evidence; automatic demotion only within an admitted reduce-risk policy. | BE-013, BE-022, BE-028, BE-035, BE-036 |
| VAN-CX-015 | Typed proof sufficiency and news arming readiness: Keep duplicate rejection, exact transition allowlists and approval/submission/readback dominators. Extend proposed news SIGNED→ARMED with valid owner grant, pre-arm window, account/mandate/policy revisions, healthy required sources and unused nonce. Execution still needs the event's two-source agreement, current quote/risk and atomic nonce claim. Validate proof issuer, subject, digest, freshness and revocation at runtime; definitions do not execute checks. | BE-002, BE-017, PRE-002 |
| VAN-CX-016 | Funding and account deletion concurrency with economic accounting: Separate local unlink, archive, credential rotation and remote closure. Use durable idempotent funding intents, beneficiary/quote-bound approval, reserved outgoing collateral, debit/credit leg reconciliation and compensating reversals. Cash-flow-adjusted returns use a versioned unitization or equivalent tested convention; preserve economic drawdown separately from authorized risk resets. Fresh atomic checks prevent unlink/deletion or withdrawal racing a fill/unknown transfer. | BE-004, BE-005, BE-006, BE-007, BE-009 |
| VAN-CX-017 | Complete backend consumer catalogue before native UX: Publish a revisioned capability/action/event/error/provenance catalogue for every admitted backend feature. Each operation declares read/write authority, availability, precision, stale semantics, approval, cancellation and receipt deep links. The full catalogue, not a fixed five-flow demo, drives information architecture and all-surface contracts after BG-001. | BE-032, BG-001, UX-001, UX-003 |
| VAN-CX-018 | Single forecasting integration without a second trading engine: Fold all 48 forecast-source packet obligations into the one parent graph, preserving their IDs through explicit aliases. Implement Qlib/LightGBM, NeuralForecast and admitted Freqtrade/FreqAI-derived lifecycle capabilities, prediction-market information and replay research labs. Retain VATI as the sole risk and order authority. No-procurement scope is preserved; live event-contract orders are not introduced. | BE-013, BE-021, BE-032, VAN-ML-PKT-048, BG-001 |
| VAN-CX-019 | Source-bound evidence promotion and non-self-issued certificates: Receipt eligibility binds exact source, configuration, test command, data, device/provider scope, issuer authority, freshness and failure evidence. Structural checks only validate the specification. F15, BG-001 and release gates require independently verified signed receipts through the qualified authority; no offline document tool can issue them. Guard proof, requirement locks and manifests remain PREPARED until owner approval. | PRE-003, BQ-006, BQ-007, BG-001, QA-005 |
| VAN-CX-020 | Full tactile design and feature preservation across every context: After BG-001, customize the accepted tactile spatial material/lighting/typography to each screen purpose. | VAN-ML-REQ-064 | Point-in-time archive and live completeness: Store publication/receive/availability clocks and per-record historical cutoffs; dedupe and reconcile archive handoffs without inventing past receipt times. | VAN-ML-PKT-038 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-065 | Exact bid and ask conversion: Parse fixed-point price/quantity strings and derive YES asks from NO bids and vice versa; retain source book and executable depth. | VAN-ML-PKT-038 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-066 | Stream gap and sequence recovery: Apply sequence-aware deltas idempotently; gaps, negative resulting depth or duplicate mismatches force INVALID until full resynchronization. | VAN-ML-PKT-038 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-067 | Canonical event and proposition identity: Bind thresholds/comparators, units, timezones, official source/vintage and rules digest; distinguish exact, complement, overlapping and non-equivalent contracts. | VAN-ML-PKT-039 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-068 | Resolution and cash settlement are separate: Track occurrence, release, close, provisional outcome, dispute/cancel and final settlement separately; corrections create versions. | VAN-ML-PKT-039 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-069 | Maturity-aware contract continuity: Key probability changes by event/proposition/horizon and explicit roll rule; missing successor contracts produce missing features. | VAN-ML-PKT-040 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-070 | Coherent distributions without fabricated tails: Differentiate mutually exclusive bins from overlapping thresholds; monotonicity, mass, open-tail and adjustment policies are explicit. | VAN-ML-PKT-040 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-071 | Quality-aware market-implied estimates: Track estimator type, depth, spread, age, activity and rule coherence separately from calibration; empty books produce missing observations. | VAN-ML-PKT-040 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-072 | Freeze expectations before official information: Link each surprise to the last genuinely available pre-release snapshot and official release vintage; no future availability. | VAN-ML-PKT-041 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-073 | Two-source agreement is not independence: Preserve parent Kalshi/Polymarket liquidity/agreement rules for armed expectations; matching horizons and semantic relation required; no independent-evidence multiplication. | VAN-ML-PKT-040 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-074 | Event-grouped calibration and adequate evidence: Calibrate by declared event family/horizon/cohort with matured labels; group dependent snapshots/contracts and report effective event counts and uncertainty. | VAN-ML-PKT-041 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-075 | Preserve probability meaning: Market-implied estimate, calibrated physical-event probability, asset outcome probability and rank score are distinct types and fields. | VAN-ML-PKT-041 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-076 | Correct Qlib and neural event-feature adapters: Feed as-of event features to existing Qlib/LightGBM tasks and supported NeuralForecast historical covariates; future calendar use is versioned and not future probabilities. | VAN-ML-PKT-042 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-077 | Conditional asset response and incremental information: Estimate event-to-instrument outcomes under context, timing and horizon; compare against conventional market/news/calendar features and use OOF fusion. | VAN-ML-PKT-042 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-078 | Preserve D1–D7 and manual ownership: External information affects entries/ranking/lifecycle only through inherited rules; D7 is required for news-generated added risk; existing risk authority sizes. | VAN-ML-PKT-043 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-079 | Net executable binary-contract value: For held-to-settlement use expected payout minus actual entry costs/fees/lockup; for early exit use executable exit distribution; market price is not independent edge. | VAN-ML-PKT-043 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-080 | Full event-loss and portfolio stress: Model maximum binary outlay, related event exposures and uncertain probabilities; Kelly-style analysis remains a bounded sandbox proposal, never sizing authority. | VAN-ML-PKT-043 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-081 | Exact fees and complete settlement accounting: Use revision/series/account-specific fee rules, per-fill precision/accumulator, cash reservations and all relevant settlement/conversion costs. | VAN-ML-PKT-044 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-082 | Queue-aware feasible event execution: Model real observation/decision/order latency, depth, partial fills, queue uncertainty and cancel races; sparse data cannot prove maker profitability. | VAN-ML-PKT-044 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-083 | Payoff-proven contract consistency: Enumerate permissible resolution/void/dispute states and quantities; exact/complement matching precedes worst-case net-payoff assessment. | VAN-ML-PKT-045 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-084 | Non-atomic multi-venue risk and capital: Keep venue-specific balances, collateral/currency conversion and hedge-failure unwind costs; no instant cross-venue capital or fill assumption. | VAN-ML-PKT-045 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-085 | Inventory-aware event quoting laboratory: Implement bounded quoting policies with event-family inventory, jump risk, adverse selection and value conditional on fills; no direct live execution. | VAN-ML-PKT-046 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-086 | Maker quote lifecycle and protective withdrawal: Simulate pending/new/partial/cancel/unknown quote states, stale-feed reduction and scheduled-event withdrawal without manipulating markets. | VAN-ML-PKT-046 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-087 | Four preregistered incremental-value studies: Implement risk-filter, surprise, volatility and ranking studies with identical opportunity sets, incumbent/calendar/exposure-matched controls and frozen utility. | VAN-ML-PKT-047 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-088 | Prospective sealing and dependent uncertainty: Seal issued forecasts before outcomes, use event/time-block inference and full trial census; compare realistic lags and negative controls. | VAN-ML-PKT-047 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-089 | Complete owner projections and operator controls: Extend existing event/research/work/evidence/settings and later native surfaces with source state, expectation lineage, study/lab controls, calibration and per-use outcomes. | VAN-ML-PKT-048 |
| VAN-ML-REQ-090 | Failure isolation and truthful full-backend closure: Isolate event workers from T0 and finance; complete recovery, revocation and rollback; all new backend obligations feed existing gates without purchasing licences or fabricating access. | VAN-ML-PKT-048 |

### 16.4 Full packet contract

Every packet names source/owner/scope digests, exact files and retained implementations, consumer and production caller, data and state models, immutable authority/effect semantics, restart/unknown/failure handling, positive and intentionally failing tests, resource and privacy constraints, external provider prerequisites, rollback, independent reviewer and receipt acceptance. Bounded backend packets may compose existing services; no partial showcase implementation substitutes for their full exit contract.

### 16.5 No-feature-loss proof

All 18 Product Truths, 93 Rev2 features, 171 atoms, 115 Rev2 contexts, 78 historical surfaces and 349 owner-action entries remain explicitly mapped. Twelve consolidated contexts extend the 216-context parent. Independent semantic admission is not inferred from populated fields. Missing callers, false source state or a skipped required capability block the responsible gate. The five historical early UI flows remain useful later UX regression journeys only; early engineering integration is headless.

## 17. DIAL FFDRM Gates and Distinct Evidence Stages

**No gate is issued or asserted qualified by publishing this blueprint.** The DIAL registry IDs below are retained; F13/F15 have their corrected predevelopment meaning.

| Gate | Predevelopment evidence contract |
|---|---|
| `F0_OWNER_INTENT_PRODUCT_TRUTH` | Exact latest owner backend-first direction, original design hashes, signed final scope/decisions and full feature coverage. |
| `F1_DEVELOPMENT_UNIT_DECOMPOSITION` | Mixed duties split, all backend work explicit, every frontend unit behind BG-001, no orphan producer. |
| `F2_FEATURE_SURFACE_COVERAGE` | All source/action/surface obligations reconciled before F15; parity baseline and falsification contract specified. Implemented oracle in BE-001, then progressively checks all completed units without falsely requiring not-yet-built UI. |
| `F3_AUTHORITY_MODEL` | Every account/trade/task/payment action has owning service, canonical bound intent, permitted proof and independent readback; no renderer/model authority. |
| `F4_STATE_PERSISTENCE_MODEL` | Versioned exact state/proof machine, failure/race/restart/idempotency and storage semantics specified for every domain. |
| `F5_CAUSAL_PATH_PROOF` | Complete planned producer/state/consumer/caller/authority/effect/readback chain; reused components separately source-evidenced. |
| `F6_RESEARCH_TOOLING_ADEQUACY` | Source/REA facts separated from inference; material architecture uncertainty resolved before affected design; tools fit proof, no false dynamic app claims. |
| `F7_ARCHITECTURAL_COHERENCE` | No backend dependency on new frontend; shared domain contracts do not import UI; no duplicate finance/agent authority. |
| `F8_FAILURE_DEGRADATION_RECOVERY` | Positive/refused/unknown/partial/recovery contracts defined; executable tests and actual backend fault evidence required later before BG-001. |
| `F9_SECURITY_PRIVACY_SECRET_BOUNDARY` | Threat model, data/secret ownership, erasure, screenshot and device policy, effect-channel separation and adversarial proof plan. |
| `F10_VERIFICATION_EVIDENCE_CONTRACT` | Source- and environment-bound backend, provider, UI, device, golden and negative evidence obligations assigned; NOT_RUN is not pass. |
| `F11_ADVERSARIAL_FORENSICS` | Independent predevelopment challenge and closed preparation blockers; preserve and rerun parent mutation counterexamples after amendment. |
| `F12_SYMBIOTIC_LOOP_PROOF` | Observe/reason/propose/authorize/act/readback/reconcile/learn edges owned; no self-promoting model or chart observation. |
| `F13_PRODUCTION_REACHABILITY` | Predevelopment runtime/external matrix specifies real environments, providers, source callers, evidence, staleness, responsible authority and promotion. It does not require the new app to exist before coding. |
| `F14_ANTI_GAP_MUTATION_PROOF` | Deleting required service/action/feature/caller, changing pins, forging grants, early frontend or bypassing settlement must fail a named gate. |
| `F15_FORENSIC_BUILD_READY_CERTIFICATION` | Independent current project-scoped certificate only after F0–F14 preparation closure, owner signature and full required pack. Precedes BE application changes; no live or UI readiness claim. |

### 17.1 Independent completion dimensions

`SPECIFICATION_CANDIDATE` is authored documentation. `FORENSIC_BUILD_READY` is independently certified predevelopment. `BACKEND_COMPLETE` is independently verified whole-backend implementation and integration, including required real external/sandbox evidence. `FRONTEND_COMPLETE`, `DEVICE_QUALIFIED`, `LIVE_ELIGIBLE` and `OWNER_ACCEPTED` require their own actual proofs. No state is inferred merely from the preceding state or a JSON flag.

No actor may issue its own independent certificate. An owner signature confirms authorized scope but does not substitute for engineering review, backend tests, provider outcomes or physical S24 proof. Document integrity pins do not prove that their author could not edit both file and pin.

### 17.2 Stage-correct qualification

Before F15: finish predevelopment semantic/evidence work, not the app. Before BG-001: finish every backend feature/service and actual integration proof, not just its specification. After BG-001: implement and qualify native visual/component/screen work. Before release: prove every applicable app-to-service path, physical interaction, both themes and owner acceptance. Before retirement: prove replacement parity and safe rollback.

## 18. Regression and adversarial validation plan

### 18.1 Foundational static checks

**Research integrity checks (OD-14, audit §3.6):** every VATI backtest and research test suite runs (1) a **look-ahead sentinel** — a fixture whose future rows are poisoned, so any strategy or feature that reads the future fails the test — and (2) a **network kill-switch**, so tests cannot silently fetch live data. Both checks are proven by deliberately breaking them once (a feature that peeks one bar ahead must fail; a test that opens a socket must fail).

Enforce no imports of legacy presentation packages in `next` screens; permitted typed domain imports registered and hashed; all 78 historic screen IDs route/host to a new equivalent or owner-approved retirement record; every owner action has a new visible entry, actual backend binding, disallowed state and readback. Enforce no inline production screenshot fixtures, fake P&L, fake task % or hardcoded operational count; all hardcoded style literals are token-owned and approved by visual authority. Compose previews cannot enter release with sample data visible as live.

### 18.2 High-risk adversarial cases

| Scenario | Expected result |
|---|---|
| Source quote stops but candle renderer continues | `STALE`/`OFFLINE`, no synthetic tick or green LIVE |
| Fast critical risk event arrives while orb celebrates a result | Critical preempts, accessible text and hub risk row; positive cue terminates |
| Two notifications share same canonical ID | One owner-visible identity across attention, hub and Android notifications |
| Orb tapped while A4 approval is pending | Opens summary; exact-action sheet entered deliberately; no grant from tap |
| Device rotates with popup open | Restored composition, correct anchor and safe navigation |
| Graph node moved on screen | Layout change only, no task mutation |
| Task source terminal error after SSE header | Terminal unavailable, bounded reconnect and no false completion |
| Muse provider approval invalid/stale lease | Refused with provider-state evidence; no click/retry side effect |
| Two agents claim same task | Visual conflict/ownership displayed; no manufactured unique assignment |
| PR #93 feature absent from merged main | Found in source-reconciliation register, not accidentally deleted |
| News article revised after model summary | Stale derivation invalidated, new verification required |
| LLM invents stop/probability/lot | Claim rejected; deterministic facts only |
| Manual entry with `owner.manual` absent from mandate | Refused before preview, reason shown |
| Manual entry requested above the strategy budget | Impossible in UI; API request refused by Risk Authority |
| Owner drags manual stop wider | Refused; stop stays; pre-trade stop integrity also refuses |
| Manual order sent | Exactly one venue request carrying order, stop and (if set) TP; no separate stop request on the normal path |
| Stop too close for the venue's stop or freeze level | Caught in preview; re-priced; known-invalid stops are blocked locally; a new broker-side constraint produces a truthful rejection and no false success |
| Fill reported without stop confirmation | Position re-read; stop re-attached (max two attempts, seconds); closed only if still unprotected; every step recorded |
| Add to a manual trade | Same flow as entry; family risk (existing + add) checked against the manual budget; `max_positions_per_instrument` enforced |
| Manual add to an automatic trade whose strategy has `NO_SCALING` or whose scale-policy conditions fail | Refused, naming the failed condition |
| Hand to VAN with an adopting strategy whose invalidation is wider than the owner's stop | Owner's stop kept; never widened |
| Take back while VAN has a pending protection action | Unsent VAN action cancelled; in-flight action reconciled; new management epoch fences further automation; completion shown only after readback |
| Handed-over trade closes | Counted for account risk and management record; excluded from strategy entry statistics |
| Manual entry while kill switch / drawdown tier 0 / blackout | Entry refused with reason; close, partial close, tighten and TP still work |
| Double tap on Review order confirm | One order (idempotency key) |
| Biometric token replayed or used after 60 s or for a different intent | Refused (act + subject + single use) |
| Close response lost | `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`; broker position checked; no blind resend |
| Pending order becomes ineligible (limits, blackout, mandate change) | VATI attempts cancel or replace before fill. Both interleavings are tested: cancel acknowledged before fill → `CANCELLED`; fill before cancel → the broker-confirmed `PARTIAL`/`FILLED` is kept, exposure reconciled, authorised protection/reduce-only applied, Attention raised. Cancellation is never claimed without broker confirmation; nothing is resent on an unknown outcome |
| Manual trade outcome | Counted in account risk; excluded from capsule learning and promotion |
| News materiality 1.0 on a T1 source (path D1) | New-trade multiplier exactly 0.25, never lower; D1 alone never closes, reduces or enlarges an open position |
| D4/D5 in shadow mode | Emits *would-have* evidence only; no position changes |
| D4/D5 promoted live (owner-signed per path) | May reduce or exit **VAN-managed** positions per their lifecycle; **owner-managed** positions only receive an alert |
| Model rewrite of a brief adds a price | Rewrite rejected, template text shown |
| Behaviour flags with fewer than the minimum closed manual trades | No flag shown; "not enough history" |
| Pattern extraction run on winners only | Refused by the extractor; losers must be included |
| Extracted pattern fails deflated Sharpe, PBO or walk-forward | Stays a research note; never reaches paper |
| Prediction market below the liquidity floor, or Polymarket unreachable | Not used; expectation falls back to consensus with the reason shown |
| Velocity spike with no trade-relevant story | Ranking change only; no trade, no D-path action |
| Strategy feature reads one bar ahead | Look-ahead sentinel test fails |
| A consolidated PR feature removed from the new route registry | Parity checker fails with `FEATURE_LOSS` |
| New UI imports `design/VanTokens` | Compilation fails (not on classpath) |
| WebView chart tries raw order command | Denied by bridge/native boundary, negative test passes |
| Broker order response lost, action might have executed | `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`, inspect exact receipt, no repeat order |
| Android Floating service deliberately stopped | No unwanted resurrection after restart |
| Low memory or TalkBack active | Orb/diagram degrade visually but preserve semantics and controls |

### 18.3 Testing layers

- Kotlin reducer/property tests for orb, source events, gestures, graph layout and models.
- Android Compose UI tests for theme, navigation, popup, touch targets, chart/positions/task focus and screen states.
- Contract tests with real gateway DTOs for positive/negative source schemas, expiry, idempotency, deep links and owner action readback.
- Backend/VATI integration tests for source-normalisation, exact receipt identity, risk/protection semantics and no shadow trading logic in Android.
- Browser/Muse/ARTEMIS E2E with real provider sessions, signed approvals and explicit denial/timeout/recovery.
- Replay deterministic tests for historical trade timelines and task event progress.
- Screenshot/animation frame golden comparisons against owner-approved art in dark/light, portrait/landscape, small/large text and low-budget modes.
- Physical S24 Ultra tests with launcher installation, signed identity, cold start, wake/dock, notification→deep link, keyboard, rotation, standby, process kill, network transitions, overlay permissions and real performance traces.
- Independent adversarial review before every canonical protected-branch admission.

### 18.4 Test-fixture hygiene

Synthetic market prices and project data are permitted **only** under test/preview labels. Screenshot fixtures must carry `DEMO / NOT LIVE` and cannot be integrated into release production state. Tests against real execution services must use safely provisioned demo/read-only contexts unless distinct live operation authorisation exists. Anti-regression suite checks that preview module dependencies are absent from production artefacts.

## 19. Deterministic backend-first migration and rollback

### 19.1 Sequence and completion

| Phase | Work | Required exit |
|---|---|---|
| P0 | PRE-001 source inventory; PRE-002 complete semantic and authority contracts; PRE-003 independent F15 | All preparation blockers closed; signed scope and qualified verifier, not a new APK |
| P1 | BE-001 source consolidation/parity, then every backend and integrated forecast/evaluator/strategy-development unit in dependency order | Every declared backend operation fully implemented with correct registered callers and service tests |
| P2 | All backend verification, including providers, execution/risk, research evaluator, cross-domain recovery and independent whole-product audit | Real scope-bound backend evidence; no required adapter or state machine hidden as future frontend work |
| Backend boundary | BG-001, the sole Global Backend Complete certificate | All required backend scope and its integration proven for the declared target; no frontend-design entry otherwise |
| P3 | UX-001/002/003 full-product information architecture, native locked-theme goldens and every-screen contracts | The complete backend informs every native interaction and presentation |
| P4 | FE-001–023 complete native implementation, including all retained and new contexts | Typed adapters, full action reachability and failure states, no domain logic smuggled into UI |
| P5 | QA-001–005 source-bound E2E, security, physical S24 and dark/light owner acceptance | Real signed-app/device/provider outcomes, independent review and complete coverage |
| P6 | REL-001 controlled signed rollout; REL-002 reviewed legacy retirement | Tested rollback, preserved financial history and zero hidden route/action loss |

Headless service integration starts early inside backend units; this is not delayed until the last backend packet. No new orb/theme/app-shell/chart renderer or native UX prototype starts early. Existing app/source inspection and baseline testing may support preservation without becoming new UI implementation. Any new backend gap found during UX reopens its backend contract and invalidates the affected global baseline before frontend compensation is allowed.

### 19.2 Branch, merge and source policy

The exact Opus branch implementations remain available at the pinned head. Consolidation of the blueprint is not approval to merge all PR101 application changes. The source admission register distinguishes retain/adapt/reference and defines repair/verification gates. Use one owner-admitted implementation lineage with bounded reviewable commits; preserve original paths and history until the complete replacement is independently proven. No bulk deletion, silent force push or blanket merge is authorized by this document.

### 19.3 Compatible release and recovery

Keep the admitted application ID, signing and pairing continuity unless a separately approved migration proves a change necessary. Feature flags select whole coherent surfaces, not mixed old/new view trees. Store changes are forward/backward compatible through the cutover window; do not downgrade secrets or lose pending provider references during rollback. Old renderers retire only after all retained functions and deep links have proof. Financial events, candidate lineage and owner decisions are never removed to make a rollback appear clean.

---

## 20. Complete delivery package and non-waivable closure

The canonical deliverable is one master blueprint, one machine plan and rendered sequence, one consolidated requirement/source/action/context mapping, full backend and consumer contracts, corrected state/financial/research obligations, locked visual resources, source admission register, exact evidence provenance, reproducible validators and a deterministic package manifest. Historical source packs are retained only under lineage/reference with their original identities.

### 20.1 What is closed by this consolidation

Source identity and the current plan conflict are reconciled. The current Opus head has **77**, not 71 or 72, VAN-NX requirement rows; OD-37–39 and their source implementation/evaluation work are retained. The whole-product backend-first schedule incorporates all forecast subpacket obligations. The scope model preserves inherited requirements/actions/contexts. Exact-source focused tests and the original plan/state self-tests were independently rerun on `van-trading-core`. Mechanical full-source and selected REA evidence remain available under their previous hashes.

### 20.2 What remains evidence-dependent—not accepted as a limitation

- Independent semantic review of all retained feature-bearing functions, 171-atom mappings, 115-context alignment and later branch candidates remains required at PRE-001/002. Populated mappings and a hash inventory do not establish that semantic proof.
- New source corrections in VAN-CX-003–016 are **specified, not implemented here**. The pinned Opus code still needs the named pending-order, limit-fill, signed-policy and evaluator repairs before the relevant backend units can close.
- Both F15 and BG-001 are **NOT_ISSUED**. An offline validator cannot sign them. The signature/issuer/scope/freshness checks belong to the qualified independent authority.
- Original REA study was static. Additional account-method source evidence closes a narrow earlier gap but does not prove broker APIs or real money movement. Dynamic reference-app study is required when material to a design decision; new VAN device proof occurs after implementation.
- Live STOP support and account/funds provider capabilities are admitted per actual venue. A missing required integration blocks its declared gate; an optional/unavailable provider must be displayed truthfully and cannot be falsely called qualified.
- Forecast research software and schemas are mandatory. No-procurement/no live event-contract executor scope remains. Complete software can correctly abstain; economic edge is not fabricated, and missing required source integration is not called runtime-qualified.
- New signed APK, physical S24, native golden, performance and live-authority evidence cannot exist before new implementation. Their P5/P6 gates are mandatory later; they are not waived by design freeze.
- OQ-05 exact signature, source-policy risk choices and provider/live decisions remain scoped to the owner. OQ-21 numerical defaults are settled; their runtime activation is not implicitly signed.
- The SSH permissions issue is a separately authorized operation. Use a qualified working route for unrelated evidence work; do not accept or silently broaden host privileges as part of frontend design.

Every open item has its first mandatory gate and owner in `verification/GAP_CLOSURE_REGISTER.json` and the runtime matrix. B4 is **engineering closure**, never 'accept incomplete work'. No feature is retired by silence or parked behind an undocumented permanent defer flag.

---

## 21. Final success definition

**VAN Next is accepted only when** the owner recognises the accepted dark/light tactile spatial identity in every relevant app surface; Home's metallic orb responds honestly to activity and risk without a legend; tapping it opens only a compact, useful navigation/notification surface; Trading Home exposes five clear destinations and full expert chart/order functions remain accessible through safe professional screens; News provides full source-backed analysis; Work/Projects expose live, verifiable dependency/agent flow; Floating VAN and all retained capabilities still work; and each action, event, stale state, broker result, task completion, approval and automation effect can be independently explained and traced to its actual authority and evidence.

**Project completion is not screen completion.** A screen can be beautiful and still lack its source, callback, authority gate, failure mode, device proof, recovery or owner acceptance. No attractive render, model assertion or generated document replaces that chain. Implementation may improve as evidence improves; the two locked owner-approved designs and functional safety obligations remain the decision anchors unless the owner explicitly changes them.

---

## 22. Complete backend architecture and the global frontend gate

### One global frontend entry gate

BG-001 is the only gateway into new native UX, visual prototypes, theme, orb/chart renderers, app shell or frontend implementation. Every required backend capability is included, not merely those consumed by the first screens. Its exact set is the 82 backend IDs and 12 backend-verification IDs in `plan/DEVELOPMENT_PLAN.json`. Logical service units do not prescribe separate deployable microservices. Forecast component qualification supplies evidence to this gate, never an alternative frontend authorization.

**Entry:** the independently issued project/source-bound PRE-003 F15 certificate is current; all required backend units are FULL, independently evidenced and integrated; all shared consumers and failure/recovery paths have passed. Requirement/source identity and economic correctness take precedence over a count of passing tests.

**No waiver:** fixtures, documentation PASS, source indexes, an old CI run, a single green happy path or a UI showcase are not completion. A genuine certificate covering a strict subset of required services is invalid. Every requirement and accepted action must be traced through the actual registered producer, state store, caller, effect and readback.

### Bound scope and evidence

A BG certificate binds project ID; canonical blueprint, plan, scope-lock and source/dependency hashes; deployed backend configuration and all consumer-contract revisions; exact required capability IDs and their operation-contract digests; every required test/result receipt; independent issuer identity and verification policy; issue/expiry/revocation data; and all unresolved or external findings. Missing, stale, duplicate, mismatched or self-issued evidence is refused.

Signature verification is performed against the independently established DIAL trust store, not a public key supplied inside the certificate, a `signature_valid` flag or the developer's self-report. This package contains no issuing credential. Its NOT_ISSUED templates cannot be promoted by running a documentation checker. The portable readiness check refuses them until genuine signed artifacts are furnished.

F15 certifies the design/implementation preparation and declared external qualification matrix. BG certifies actual whole-backend completion within the explicitly admitted deployment target. Neither grants live orders, funds movement, provider access or Android release authority.

### Complete consumer catalogue

BE-032 exposes a versioned capability/action catalogue: operation and subject IDs, account/strategy/source scope, input/output schemas, state machines, source freshness, eligibility/refusal reasons, required owner proof, event cursors, outcome lookup, privacy and display-field semantics. It includes account/funds, risk/lockout scopes, pending-order/position outcomes, model/strategy development, forecast/uncertainty, event research labs, all Work/Memory/Automation/Browser/Voice/Muse/ARTEMIS controls and other preserved functions.

Headless consumers exercise every supported operation and denial path before UX begins. A backend field being serializable does not prove the correct client actor can call it, an owner action can be executed, or a false result is rejected. No generated-client or fixture shortcut substitutes for the real service registration and actual permitted provider path required by that capability.

### External qualification and no-procurement boundary

Forecast/model/contract research software is implemented in full with admitted existing data or labelled scenarios, as directed. There is no new subscription, license procurement, provider account creation or live prediction-market order sender in this programme. A research-only lab can be qualified for its declared offline/replay purpose without claiming live-market admission.

An operation whose target contract requires a real broker/payment sandbox, data feed, provider receipt or other actual external behavior remains NOT_QUALIFIED until that evidence exists. It cannot be silently reclassified as offline to pass BG. Unrelated backend engineering can proceed in dependency order, but BG cannot waive an unresolved mandatory integration in the approved target. An explicit owner-approved scope amendment is required to change the target, with new evidence and no deletion of historical obligations.

### Reopening and frontend feedback

Detailed native UX begins only after BG. Backend interface ergonomics and domain semantics are specified/tested beforehand; these are not UI prototypes. Once UX begins, a genuinely missing backend capability, changed authority or incompatible contract reopens BG, updates the source/scope fingerprints, invalidates affected receipts and pauses affected frontend work. Implement and requalify the backend change before resuming. No domain logic is hidden inside a screen to keep an obsolete gate green.

### Required falsification cases

Delete a backend unit or operation, remove an inherited owner action, omit forecast component evidence, forge a result, change a source hash, revoke a grant, submit a stale provider receipt, substitute synthetic cash settlement, relabel an incomplete backend unit as frontend, or add a UX prerequisite path that bypasses BG: the applicable validator and real promotion check must refuse. Every refusal has an evidence ID and reason. This document specifies those obligations; it does not certify their runtime implementation.

### Full backend service catalogue

### BE-001 — Source consolidation and preservation oracle
**Prerequisites:** PRE-003
**Authority:** VAN source owners plus independent Zuul admission
**Models:** source revisions, adoption ledger, legacy action and endpoint identities

**Complete operations:**
- admit exact feature-bearing diffs
- retain old app unchanged
- register every provider caller
- construct and falsify parity oracle

**Adversarial exit:** Remove a PR-only action: parity fails; unreviewed duplicate implementation cannot merge
**Corrections:** VAN-CX-002

### BE-002 — Device identity, bounded authority and intent fencing
**Prerequisites:** BE-001, PRE-003
**Authority:** VAN gateway security and existing device authority
**Models:** OwnerPrincipal, DeviceBinding, CanonicalIntent, Grant, RevocationEpoch

**Complete operations:**
- pair/provision/revoke
- validate signed exact intents
- issue challenges
- expire and fence sessions
- protect account and task scopes

- Exercise Netcup/Oracle host profiles, endpoint changes, stream gaps, source revocation, disk/backlog pressure, restart, duplicate/unknown publication and schema rollback with no T0/provider network coupling.
- Provide honest per-source integration state and no-procurement development path; all mandatory backend/parent obligations remain visible and source-specific real-data gates cannot be replaced by fixtures.

**Adversarial exit:** Headless real-service positive/recovery/error readbacks; all new requirements join independent packet032 closure and parent033/BG-001.; No background order enablement, licence purchase, new account, cloud bill, fixture-to-live promotion or false full-provider-ready claim.
**Corrections:** all canonical source/proof/authority invariants


## 23. Backend-driven, owner-designed every-screen experience

### Stable context identity

Every context carries project/owner scope, selected account ID (never inferred from aggregate ALL), canonical instrument and venue, source epoch/revision, event/received time, validity/freshness, permission revision and an explicit authoritative outcome. Use opaque IDs, exact quantities, currency and unit type. Display-rounded numbers never re-enter risk calculations.

### Required per-capability contract

`capability_id`, `owner_requirement_refs`, `domain_owner`, `purpose`, `allowed_contexts`, `read_operations`, `effect_operations`, `inputs`, `outputs`, `validation`, `authorization`, `state_machine_ref`, `persistence`, `idempotency_scope`, `receipt_lookup`, `recovery`, `rate_limit`, `source_age_rules`, `provider_support`, `version`, `deprecation`, `tests`, `source_binding`, `runtime_proof_refs`.

Every effect operation declares preview, argument digest, exact owner/device proof where required, eligibility read, domain execution, idempotency/outbox and reconciliation lookup. Neither a model nor renderer can call raw broker or infrastructure commands.

### Required per-action presentation data

`action_id`, `capability_id`, `subject_ref`, `context_digest`, `classification NAVIGATE|READ|DRAFT|OWNER_EFFECT`, `eligibility AVAILABLE|REVIEW_REQUIRED|STALE|NOT_SUPPORTED|UNKNOWN`, reason code and short text, exact proposed effect, source time, visible units, semantic warning, expected state transitions and cancellation semantics. The app cannot enable a financial action simply because its control was rendered.

The server supplies stable semantic keys and verified facts, not HTML/JavaScript that can mint grants or a compulsory grid of every internal field. UI text is generated from approved templates or checked facts. Security metadata, hashes and low-level request bodies stay in expandable evidence surfaces, not Home.

### Snapshot and event contract

Use `source_id`, `stream_id`, `epoch`, `sequence`, `event_id`, `occurred_at`, `received_at`, `snapshot_revision`, `subject_id`, `payload_schema`, `freshness`, `correlation_id` and `authority_effect`. A reconnect obtains a versioned snapshot and subsequent ordered events. Duplicates do not animate again or submit again. A cursor gap or incomparable epoch does not merge old and new truth.

Trading and work domains keep independent revisions. A global badge cannot claim all sources LIVE because market prices are fresh while positions or task ownership are stale. Tick coalescing may reduce redraw frequency, never drop meaningful order, approval or ledger transitions.

### Native versus backend ownership

| Concern | Complete in backend phase | Complete after BG-001 |
|---|---|---|
| Chart | Instrument/bar/provenance APIs; canonical settings/drawings/indicator models; bridge schema; research intake; replay namespace | Renderer, crosshair/gesture/surface lifecycle, native/web host, local viewport/store and accessible controls |
| Orb | Orthogonal presence sources, severity reducer policy, freshness, canonical attention/briefing projections | Metallic render, motion, hit targets, Home composition, compact popup and accessible captions |
| Tasks | Authoritative graph, assignments, stage progress, handoff/lease events and actions | Spatial layout/gestures, node selection, mini-map, list alternative and tactile hierarchy |
| Accounts/funds | Full identity/auth/quote/approval/operation/reconciliation/capability services | Native forms, verified provider handoff chrome, receipt/detail/protection UI |
