| Voice/device | Server protocol, policy, stored consent, existing qualified device adapter interface and test vectors | New Android mic/overlay/notification/provisioning UX and exact S24 permission/lifecycle proof |
| Muse/browser | Real scoped job/session/approval broker with provider readback | Native conversation/control panel and viewport touch interaction |

### Headless consumer acceptance before frontend

For every action, exercise the genuine registered gateway contract from an isolated authorized test client. Validate exact scope, messages, real dependent services, positive/refusal/unknown outcomes and readback. Unit tests and fake endpoints remain development aids, never the sole integration evidence. Headless tests must not skip authentication because the mobile signing UI is not built; use an already qualified signing/test authority whose scope excludes live effects where not approved.

Include complete contract data for every feature, not just five representative flows. Representative flows are risk-discovery probes in BQ; they cannot certify untested sibling capabilities.

### Consolidated model/research/execution extensions

Every target screen resolves against the same completed BE-032 catalogue. The additional model registry, calibrated expectations, candidate trial history, source/evaluator qualification, risk-policy activation and pending-order recovery contexts are declared in ALL_SURFACE_CONTRACTS.json. Model scores, event-market probabilities and DSR/PBO statistics have different semantics and labels; none can directly create a trade. Global hard risk covers all origins, while discretionary and autonomous cooldown scopes are displayed distinctly. Order submission, venue working, fill, protection confirmation and terminal cancellation/expiry remain different states.

## 24. Financial, state and research clarifications

### 1. Armed ticket readiness is not event confirmation

Proposed minimum `SIGNED → ARMED` readiness proof: verified owner/device signature, canonical grant digest, permitted pre-arm time window, correct event instance, active account/mandate, current stop/execution-policy revisions, healthy required feed connections, unused nonce, and no revocation or conflicting arming. Store the readiness snapshot and expiry. **Do not require the future actual release values before arming**, which would destroy the intended pre-armed feature.

`ARMED → USED` additionally requires the actual event/time conditions, both required independent sources delivered and agreeing where OD-13/OD-24 applies, fresh quote/spread/risk, current account/mandate/policy, and atomic nonce claim tied to a durable intent. Source-health deterioration or scope drift disarms/requires fresh validation. A signed but not ready ticket is displayed as signed/not armed, not executable.

The inherited approved transition list is archived by exact parent source. Adding this stronger readiness proof requires a separately proposed amended transition/guard lock and its owner/independent admission; it must not silently repin a signed parent file. BQ-003 must mutate each readiness element and observe failure. The source text's official-first exception for unscheduled headlines must be reconciled explicitly against its two-source acceptance tests: until resolved, **do not authorize single-source execution**.

### 2. Pending orders and protection

An order request includes its protective stop; that does not guarantee every venue accepted a stop or every later fill remains protected. Protection states are `REQUESTED`, `BROKER_CONFIRMED` and `UNCONFIRMED/UNKNOWN`, with source revision and exact position. Recovery re-reads, re-attaches where admitted, and invokes the authorized risk-reduction fallback at its deadline. Network failure prevents a success claim; a deadline schedules/escalates a bounded action rather than guaranteeing it succeeded within seconds.

Risk reservations cover resting and simultaneous sibling orders. Cancellation or modification can lose a race with a fill; the broker event wins and is reconciled. A take-back increments the management epoch and fences new automatic actions, but any already-sent action is reconciled rather than represented as magically cancelled. The final owner-visible status states which operations remain in flight.

### 3. Full monetary units and flow adjustment

Money, fees, collateral and executed quantities use integer minor units or declared decimal scale and currency/contract identity; conversions use authoritative timestamped rates and explicit rounding. Risk sizing includes contract value, price distance, quote/account conversion and venue steps—never a universal lots = currency risk / raw price-distance shortcut.

A deposit or withdrawal is an external cash flow, not P&L. Adopt a specified, tested flow-adjusted performance method. For a unitized baseline, a verified external flow at valuation NAV issues/redeems units at that NAV; trading return comes from NAV changes, not unit count. Alternatively use correctly timed subperiod returns. The accounting policy must define valuation ordering, simultaneous trade fills/fees, credits, reversals, transfers, negative or zero capital and unresolved flows. Synthetic examples are test fixtures, not account reports.

Removing cash should not fabricate a trading loss, but it can legitimately reduce collateral and increase exposure relative to remaining capital. Therefore tests distinguish flow-neutral performance from margin/risk eligibility: a withdrawal that would under-margin open exposure is refused; flow-adjusted historical drawdown does not grant spendable cash. Deposits never erase prior trading losses. Non-withdrawable credit is not risk capital by default. Unknown movements are quarantined for reconciliation, not guessed.

### 4. Effective-once effects under uncertainty

“Exactly once” is the intended business invariant, not an assertion that a network offers universal exactly-once delivery. Persist intent and outbox atomically before external send; bind stable provider idempotency where supported; claim/record outcome; reconcile on timeout before any retry; deduplicate callbacks; preserve reversals as compensating events. Unsupported provider idempotency demands a verified reconciliation protocol. If outcome cannot be determined, keep `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` and block duplicate risk rather than falsely report success.

Withdrawal approval binds beneficiary digest, currency, amount, fees/net, quote revision, expiry and owner/account identity. Server-side transactional reservations must serialize competing withdrawal/order usage. A callback redirect or HTTP200 is not provider settlement. Transfers require separate debit and credit confirmation and partial/unknown recovery.

### 5. Learning and observations

Manual trade outcome records may include realized holding duration. Entry-time pattern extraction may not use that future value as an entry feature; only a predeclared planned horizon known at decision time is allowed. Split chronological datasets, poison future observations and block network calls in deterministic research tests. Winners and losers both remain. Diagnostic findings are descriptive; candidate strategy extraction does not promote an existing strategy or mint a grant.

### 6. Closed versus pending facts

The source file-availability gap for the pinned PR93 snapshot is closed; its causal semantic review is not. Selected MT5/TradingView account methods have been inspected; no competitor runtime or provider funding semantics were proven. Existing chart/account spike branches remain unqualified candidates. No prepared hash list is an owner signature. F15 and BG-001 remain `NOT_ISSUED` until independent evidence and real authority establish otherwise.

### Machine-readable readiness amendment

`NEWS_ARMING_READINESS_AMENDMENT_REV1_10.json` contains the exact proposed `SIGNED → ARMED` proof set, typed issuer/field definitions, preserved use proofs and explicit meaning of USED. It does not require future actual values before arming. It is not an owner-signed replacement of the parent guard list. `verification/verify_news_readiness.py` checks that the proposal has no missing or shortcut readiness guards; it cannot attest actual signatures or market inputs.

## 25. Source traceability and reproducible specification verification

The exact Opus head, master hashes, parent/additive source packages, read-only VM source-check receipts, source admission register, correction contracts and every-screen obligations are in this pack. `CANONICAL_AUTHORITY.json` names the only active master and plan. `plan/render_plan.py --check` refuses a stale rendered plan; `verification/verify_unified.py` checks locked scope, dependencies, complete mappings, backend dominance and all document contracts; `verification/verify_unified.py --selftest` demonstrates specific invalid cases. `verification/verify_package.py` verifies every distributed member. These commands never issue F15, BG or runtime approvals.

Read source evidence by immutable commit and exact path, and compare against the supplied source ledger. Historical raw REA records not present in the portable evidence ZIP remain identified by VM path and digest. Missing record content must be obtained before claiming it reviewed; metadata is not the record itself. Retained archive plans are explicitly historical, never parallel active instructions.

## 26. Definitive whole-product completion test

The product is complete only when every admitted requirement, service operation, legacy owner action, new capability and screen context has its required source, runtime and physical proof. A documented negative research result is truthful, but an unimplemented research engine is not complete. A safety refusal is legitimate, but a missing provider adapter disguised as a permanent disabled button is not fulfillment of an admitted live-capability requirement. A 74-unit backend catalogue is not proof of 74 complete capabilities; BG requires their actual evidence. The final app must preserve the locked tactile identity in every context, function from complete backend contracts, and pass owner/device acceptance before release or legacy retirement.

## 27. Source implementation reconciliation and mandatory repairs

### VAN-CX-001 — One canonical authority and backend-first completion graph
**Owners:** PRE-002, PRE-003, BG-001. **Source:** Current owner directive; Rev1.10; Opus plan/generate_plan.py

**Cause:** Multiple valid-looking versioned plans can cause scope or sequence forks.

Only CANONICAL_AUTHORITY.json selects the active master and generated plan. Historical plans are immutable lineage, never runnable implementation authority. All backend and backend-verification units precede BG-001; every new UX/frontend unit depends on the same BG-001 scope.

**Positive proof:** The generated graph and its source-scope digest determine one reproducible sequence.
**Counterexample:** Select the old mixed plan, omit a backend producer, or reclassify a chart renderer as backend: reject.
**Recovery:** A relevant source, policy or scope amendment invalidates affected receipts and BG-001 without erasing historical evidence.

### VAN-CX-002 — Source-preserving implementation admission
**Owners:** PRE-001, PRE-002, BE-001. **Source:** Opus a39f241 versus main 8eb1889; PR93/95/97/98/99 and quarantined spikes

**Cause:** A blanket branch merge or a main-only rebuild loses the distinction between valuable functions and unqualified changes.

Keep exact source paths, commit and content hashes, test evidence, feature obligations and ADOPT/ADAPT/REFERENCE dispositions. Retain all source candidates until their replacement or rejection is explicitly justified; never accept a reviewed module solely because its parent branch has passing CI.

**Positive proof:** Every changed implementation group and historical feature has a source-to-backend-to-screen-to-test chain.
**Counterexample:** Delete a PR-only action, discard a complete candidate branch without dispositions, or report an inferred effect as observed: reject.
**Recovery:** Cherry-pick or port bounded accepted changes onto the canonical implementation lineage only after F15 and source admission; keep rollback to exact source.

### VAN-CX-003 — Protection parity for immediate and pending fills
**Owners:** BE-010, BE-012, BE-035. **Source:** trading/vati/app/cycle.py::_settle_pending; trading/vati/execution/router.py immediate fill path

**Cause:** Pending fill settlement bypasses the protection-confirmation response used for immediate fills.

Route FILLED and PARTIAL receipts from all origins through one protection reconciler. Track REQUESTED, CONFIRMED, UNKNOWN and MISSING separately. Never register an intended local stop as proof of a venue stop. Apply the currently admitted immediate flatten/halt response unless an independently qualified owner-admitted bounded re-read/reattach policy replaces it; record timeout and unknown close outcomes.

**Positive proof:** A pending fill with a rejected venue stop triggers the same admitted recovery as an immediate fill and updates risk and Attention.
**Counterexample:** Inject rejected/unknown protection at delayed or partial fill; a local rule alone must not yield CONFIRMED or permit additional risk.
**Recovery:** Keep protective orders and reservation state through crash; reconcile original receipt/position before any compensating action, with no blind close retry.

### VAN-CX-004 — Limit-price invariants and explicit order semantics
**Owners:** BE-008, BE-010, BE-019, BE-035. **Source:** trading/vati/execution/paper.py::submit/_work_pending; router.py LIMIT_AT_TOUCH substitution

**Cause:** The at-touch LIMIT path executes beyond its declared price and may substitute MARKET under the same authorization.

BUY LIMIT fills at or below its limit and SELL LIMIT at or above it, using the relevant executable quote and eligible event time. An at-touch order that is not executable within its limit rests or is refused. A capped-market order is a distinct intent/template with its own signed semantics; logging a LIMIT-to-MARKET substitution does not make it authorized. Unknown venue capabilities deny the operation.

**Positive proof:** At-touch and gapped quote fixtures preserve the limit in both directions; explicit capped-market fixtures preserve their separate slippage envelope.
**Counterexample:** The current 1.10000 limit with 0.00010 spread and 0.00002 slippage must never fill a BUY at 1.10007 or a SELL at 1.09993.
**Recovery:** After a gap, respect limit/stop/stop-limit distinctions; missed fills are not fabricated to preserve historical backtest counts.

### VAN-CX-005 — Pending-order expiry and event-policy propagation
**Owners:** BE-010, BE-014, BE-016, BE-019, BE-035. **Source:** ExecutionPolicyEngine/STOP_ENTRY.max_wait_ms; OrderCommand.expires_at_ms; PaperAdapter._rest

**Cause:** Policy time-to-live and cancel-on-event constraints disappear before the resting-order worker.

Bind policy digest, order expiry, mandate validity, market-session constraints and cancel-on-event rules into the immutable execution command and pending supervisor. Effective expiry is the earliest applicable allowed deadline; absent or stale policy is not a 24-hour default. Clock and source revisions remain explicit.

**Positive proof:** A four-hour STOP_ENTRY cannot remain eligible for 24 hours; PRE_BLACKOUT/POST_BLACKOUT moves eligible pending orders into cancellation workflow.
**Counterexample:** Drop max_wait, substitute a later expiry, or fill an ineligible resting order without reconciliation/Attention: fail.
**Recovery:** Model cancel/fill and partial-fill races honestly. Reserve full remaining risk until authoritative cancellation/expiry/readback; reconcile a fill that wins the race.

### VAN-CX-006 — Terminal order read models and monotonic lifecycle reduction
**Owners:** BE-003, BE-010, BE-012, BE-032. **Source:** trading/vati/app/tradebook.py::_entry_receipt/current_trades

**Cause:** The first ACCEPTED/PENDING event is used when no later fill is found, even after a terminal event.

Project orders from the complete canonical lifecycle, ordered and deduplicated by broker event/sequence and reconciliation revision. PLACED is the code state and Working the label. Separate unfilled terminal orders from residual exposure after partial fills. Conflicting events become reconciliation-required, not last-arrival optimism.

**Positive proof:** EXPIRED, CANCELLED and REJECTED unfilled orders disappear from working-order totals but remain in historical records; partial positions retain actual exposure.
**Counterexample:** A stale ACCEPTED receipt after terminal readback must not resurrect Working; duplicate fill/cancel events must not double-count.
**Recovery:** Snapshot replay produces the same positions, orders, reservations and summaries after restart.

### VAN-CX-007 — Executable quantisation before authority and metadata-based slippage
**Owners:** BE-008, BE-009, BE-010. **Source:** RouteRegistry.quantise; policy_templates 1.1.0; mt5_pull.deviation_points

**Cause:** Post-approval rounding changes financial meaning; instrument digit guesses leak into absolute bounds.

Use provider SymbolContract tick size, point size, digits, units, min/freeze stops and revision to build the executable preview first. Validate stop side and distance, risk in account currency and lot step on exact decimals. Risk and owner approval bind those executable values. Version relative-price versus absolute-price versus points units; recalculate and reapprove if changed. Preserve the no-widening invariant but do not hide a broker-invalid too-close stop.

**Positive proof:** FX, JPY, metals and alternative broker digit fixtures produce correct bounds from actual contract metadata and remain within the approved loss envelope.
**Counterexample:** Unknown digits, stale contract revision, a rounded stop beyond risk or below broker distance, or treating an old absolute cap as a new fraction: refuse.
**Recovery:** Quote or contract changes invalidate the preview; historical mandate/template versions replay with their original units.

### VAN-CX-008 — Incremental ledger verification with explicit integrity envelope
**Owners:** BE-003, BE-009, BE-033. **Source:** Ledger.verify_incremental / IncrementalChainVerifier; Opus SQLite and Postgres tests

**Cause:** Per-decision full-ledger scans become unbounded, but a speed shortcut can weaken integrity detection.

Retain verified incremental anchors and periodic full checks; bind ledger identity, row sequence/count, chain root, source epoch and checkpoint lifetime. Full verification is required on first use, detected change, failed incremental proof and the admitted maximum interval. Document the distinction between immediate detection of new-row/rechain/deletion faults and delayed detection of in-place historical edits; stronger write protection or integrity monitoring may reduce that window.

**Positive proof:** New-row work scales with appended records while honest replay matches full verification in SQLite and Postgres.
**Counterexample:** Tamper, truncate, fork, roll back, replace a ledger or supply a stale checkpoint: fail closed and force full verification. The research always-True monkeypatch is forbidden.
**Recovery:** Checkpoints are rebuilt or verified after restart; concurrency and interrupted checkpoint writes cannot leave risk operating on untrusted state.

### VAN-CX-009 — Signed lockout policy activation and persistent all-origin risk
**Owners:** BE-002, BE-006, BE-009, BE-011, BE-013. **Source:** OD-38/39; risk/lockouts.py; AccountCoordinatorService.build and DecisionCycle

**Cause:** Accepted code defaults are consumed without reading the new fields from a signed mandate; separate cooldown scopes can be confused with global account limits.

Retain 24-hour cooldown, 0.5 probation and suspension after more than two cooldowns in seven days as the accepted values, not live authority. Bind all fields, scope rules and policy revision into the signed mandate before activating a changed production policy. Keep autonomous strategy/account and owner.manual cooldown scopes as recorded by OD-38; always apply economic all-origin account drawdown, margin, exposure and protection ceilings. Persist reset events, loss history, economic peaks and day/week baselines.

**Positive proof:** Restart replays the same locks; independent manual/autonomous cooldown tests coexist with shared all-origin hard-limit tests.
**Counterexample:** A restart resets losses, a default silently changes a signed policy, a manual entry bypasses total-account drawdown, or an owner reset erases economic loss: reject.
**Recovery:** A signed reset changes a permission state or risk reference with an audit event, never the immutable economic performance history; unknown policy/collateral blocks new risk.

### VAN-CX-010 — Qualified chronological evaluator before automated strategy development
**Owners:** BE-019, BE-035, BE-036. **Source:** Opus research/strategy_backtest_20261017/harness.py/analyze.py; owner OD-37

**Cause:** A research loop can optimize simulator defects and report invalid validation as profitable edge.

Establish one qualified evaluator version before candidate selection uses it. Separate isolation experiments from deployment-faithful evaluation; both declare code, data, controls, costs and random seeds. An independent fixture oracle checks event eligibility, bid/ask, limit/stop/expiry, partial fills, costs, cash flows and restart/risk controls.

**Positive proof:** Known-profit/loss fixtures match analytically specified results and the actual execution adapters' admitted semantics.
**Counterexample:** Disabling controls, altering quote units or a permissive fill model without a distinct experiment identity invalidates promotion evidence.
**Recovery:** An evaluator correction invalidates dependent candidate conclusions and schedules reproducible reevaluation; old results remain labeled historical.

### VAN-CX-011 — Leakage-proof chronology and genuine walk-forward records
**Owners:** BE-035, BE-036, VAN-ML-PKT-008, VAN-ML-PKT-021. **Source:** analyze.py wf_windows=len(years); harness peek=True; Rev1.10 research integrity contract

**Cause:** Yearly grouping and sensitivity to deliberately supplied future bars are mistaken for leakage-free walk-forward proof.

Persist train/validation/test intervals, strategy frozen-at time, embargo/purge rules, label horizons, warmup-only data and candidate selection cutoffs. Mutating only unavailable future data must leave prior features and decisions unchanged. A deliberately leaking implementation must fail. Yearly summaries remain descriptive, not walk-forward windows; a 2025 label does not by itself create untouched out-of-sample evidence.

**Positive proof:** Replay of each named chronological fold yields the same pre-cutoff decisions and verified split provenance.
**Counterexample:** Future-only perturbation changes past decisions, a scaler sees the holdout, or window count is only the count of calendar years: fail.
**Recovery:** Tainted fold/candidate artifacts cannot be reused as independent promotion evidence; retain audit lineage.

### VAN-CX-012 — Immutable dataset, quote, calendar and cost provenance
**Owners:** BE-008, BE-019, BE-035, VAN-ML-PKT-006. **Source:** Opus public mirror research data; bid OHLC with symmetric quote construction

**Cause:** A mirror dataset's units/time conventions and research adjustments are not independently fixed.

Use a DatasetManifest with exact hashes, provider/mirror revision, permitted-use state, timezone/DST handling, bid/ask/mid semantics, bar timestamp conventions, missing/duplicate intervals and transformation code. Preserve full spread, commissions, financing, slippage, liquidity and FX assumptions per venue and period. A source access/usage gap is never hidden by calling fixtures live.

**Positive proof:** From permitted raw inputs, a second run reproduces the same bars, sessions, costs and outcomes.
**Counterexample:** Bid is silently treated as midpoint, fixed EST becomes DST-local time, a bar is used before close or a mirror is unpinned: reject qualifying use.
**Recovery:** Keep lawful metadata and reproducibility scripts without republishing restricted data; qualifying tests await admitted data while unrelated authorized engineering continues.

### VAN-CX-013 — Complete trial ledger and honest statistical interpretation
**Owners:** BE-035, BE-036, VAN-ML-PKT-020, VAN-ML-PKT-021. **Source:** DSR/PBO output labels and pooled candidate scoring in Opus analysis

**Cause:** Selection-process statistics are described as personal skill probabilities and copied to individual instruments.

Record every tried configuration, failure, search budget, selection rule, effective trial treatment, untouched holdout and actual cost model. Label DSR and PBO according to their statistical meaning and calculation cohort, not chance of skill or trade-win probability. Economic admission is per exact dataset/evaluator/model/policy scope, separate from software completion.

**Positive proof:** Each reported metric identifies the population, formula/version, trial set, uncertainty and selection scope.
**Counterexample:** A pooled PBO is relabeled pair-specific, losers disappear from trial count, or repeated holdout tuning is called independent: fail promotion.
**Recovery:** A no-qualified-candidate result is valid; do not search until a cherry-picked pass can be manufactured.

### VAN-CX-014 — Governed weekly strategy-development lifecycle
**Owners:** BE-013, BE-022, BE-028, BE-035, BE-036. **Source:** OD-37; VAN-NX-072; existing learning/evolution/validation services

**Cause:** Unscheduled existing capabilities and ambiguous per-transition approval undermine a coherent automated research loop.

One standing, scoped research grant may schedule offline diagnostics, generate candidate versions and evaluate them; it cannot alter live policy. Loss clusters generate hypotheses evaluated against winners, exposure/opportunity frequency and market regimes. Separate distribution drift from predictive deterioration. Preserve parents and all trials. Passing candidates appear as promotion proposals; failures and system faults stay inspectable. Live promotion uses a fresh exact artifact/policy-bound owner decision and stage evidence; automatic demotion only within an admitted reduce-risk policy.

**Positive proof:** A weekly run is idempotent by period/strategy/dataset/evaluator; results reproduce and a candidate reaches only permitted next states.
**Counterexample:** A model edits protected execution/risk paths, repeated runs duplicate studies, a failed candidate becomes a promotion opportunity, or an unsigned candidate trades live: refuse.
**Recovery:** Resume interrupted jobs from durable checkpoints; outdated evaluations are invalidated, not overwritten; current production champion remains unchanged.

### VAN-CX-015 — Typed proof sufficiency and news arming readiness
**Owners:** PRE-002, BE-002, BE-017. **Source:** Opus 159-transition guard list and 35 proof types; uncovered SIGNED to ARMED gap; Rev1.10 readiness amendment

**Cause:** A locked transition list can preserve an insufficient guard, while a proof label alone does not establish a live fact.

Keep duplicate rejection, exact transition allowlists and approval/submission/readback dominators. Extend proposed news SIGNED→ARMED with valid owner grant, pre-arm window, account/mandate/policy revisions, healthy required sources and unused nonce. Execution still needs the event's two-source agreement, current quote/risk and atomic nonce claim. Validate proof issuer, subject, digest, freshness and revocation at runtime; definitions do not execute checks.

**Positive proof:** A pre-signed ticket arms only in its declared readiness envelope and can claim the nonce once.
**Counterexample:** Empty proof, arbitrary real proof label, proof for another account/event, re-signed weakened allowlist or concurrent duplicate use: reject.
**Recovery:** Arming and consuming a grant are not a broker fill; unknown submission follows the order reconciler, never a second order.

### VAN-CX-016 — Funding and account deletion concurrency with economic accounting
**Owners:** BE-004, BE-005, BE-006, BE-007, BE-009. **Source:** OD-18/25; Accounts & Funds ownership planes; prior F-033; Opus local registry candidates

**Cause:** Local account actions, money settlement and trading collateral can be conflated or checked against inconsistent snapshots.

Separate local unlink, archive, credential rotation and remote closure. Use durable idempotent funding intents, beneficiary/quote-bound approval, reserved outgoing collateral, debit/credit leg reconciliation and compensating reversals. Cash-flow-adjusted returns use a versioned unitization or equivalent tested convention; preserve economic drawdown separately from authorized risk resets. Fresh atomic checks prevent unlink/deletion or withdrawal racing a fill/unknown transfer.

**Positive proof:** Deposit/withdrawal/credit/fee and pending-order interleavings reconcile exact money, positions and risk without false P&L.
**Counterexample:** Redirect settles money, a timeout resubmits, deleting restores secrets, unclassified flow silently improves risk capital, or a withdrawal bypasses concurrent reservations: reject.
**Recovery:** Rollback never erases financial events; credential cancellation returns RELINK_REQUIRED and broker closure remains independently verified.

### VAN-CX-017 — Complete backend consumer catalogue before native UX
**Owners:** BE-032, BG-001, UX-001, UX-003. **Source:** Latest owner backend-first instruction; whole-product B1 coverage

**Cause:** Screens designed around partial endpoints omit later functions or implement authority in Android.

Publish a revisioned capability/action/event/error/provenance catalogue for every admitted backend feature. Each operation declares read/write authority, availability, precision, stale semantics, approval, cancellation and receipt deep links. The full catalogue, not a fixed five-flow demo, drives information architecture and all-surface contracts after BG-001.

**Positive proof:** Every mapped owner action and new capability has one traceable caller and appropriate screen/sheet/dialog outcome.
**Counterexample:** A visually complete screen lacks its source or any backend is unfinished; BG-001 and frontend entry refuse.
**Recovery:** A newly discovered missing backend capability reopens its backend unit and invalidates affected BG/UX proof before front-end compensation.

### VAN-CX-018 — Single forecasting integration without a second trading engine
**Owners:** BE-013, BE-021, BE-032, BG-001, VAN-ML-PKT-048. **Source:** VAN Forecast Adoption Rev1.1 parent delta and current owner full-feature requirement

**Cause:** A complete child plan left outside the parent creates parallel authorities or missing backend dependencies.

Fold all 48 forecast-source packet obligations into the one parent graph, preserving their IDs through explicit aliases. Implement Qlib/LightGBM, NeuralForecast and admitted Freqtrade/FreqAI-derived lifecycle capabilities, prediction-market information and replay research labs. Retain VATI as the sole risk and order authority. No-procurement scope is preserved; live event-contract orders are not introduced.

**Positive proof:** Every one of the 90 forecast requirements maps to an active unit and test; all required child backend receipts reach BG-001.
**Counterexample:** A child F15/BG independently opens UI, Qlib or Freqtrade sends a live order, or a unavailable provider is labeled healthy: reject.
**Recovery:** Data/model failure yields explicit abstention or unavailable state; no economically qualified model is a permissible result, not permission to omit software.

### VAN-CX-019 — Source-bound evidence promotion and non-self-issued certificates
**Owners:** PRE-003, BQ-006, BQ-007, BG-001, QA-005. **Source:** DIAL PRD-DDP-R2 F13–F15 and Rev1.9b evidence builder

**Cause:** A deterministic plan or local PASS field is mistaken for independent execution/production proof.

Receipt eligibility binds exact source, configuration, test command, data, device/provider scope, issuer authority, freshness and failure evidence. Structural checks only validate the specification. F15, BG-001 and release gates require independently verified signed receipts through the qualified authority; no offline document tool can issue them. Guard proof, requirement locks and manifests remain PREPARED until owner approval.

**Positive proof:** The complete pack rebuilds identically and invalid evidence cannot change readiness.
**Counterexample:** Edit input and matching local hash, delete a receipt, swap source revision, self-declare signature_valid, or reuse device evidence across builds: block readiness.
**Recovery:** Record revoked/stale evidence; regenerate affected planning fingerprints and rerun relevant tests without erasing old records.

### VAN-CX-020 — Full tactile design and feature preservation across every context
**Owners:** UX-002, UX-003, FE-023, QA-001, QA-004. **Source:** Locked dark/light references, 216 inherited contexts and consolidated research/account changes

**Cause:** Backend completeness alone does not guarantee coherent native presentation or complete feature reachability.

After BG-001, customize the accepted tactile spatial material/lighting/typography to each screen purpose. Preserve every context and 349 historical action entries, add strategy/forecast/execution-state views where needed, and retain accessible alternatives. A graph, chart workstation, funding confirmation and small orb hub share design DNA, not one repeated generic card layout.

**Positive proof:** Full-scope native dark/light goldens, action reachability and physical S24 cases match the same backend catalogue revision.
**Counterexample:** Only showcase flows pass, legacy UI leaks into new modules, safety states are hidden for aesthetics, or unknown data looks live: reject final qualification.
**Recovery:** Reduce motion/material cost gracefully without removing semantics; preserve signed identity and reversible cutover until every required feature is verified.

## 28. Integrated forecasting, quant research and adaptive model services

The existing Forecast Adoption Rev1.1 is merged into this parent rather than left as a separate executable programme. All 48 source packet obligations, 90 requirements, 180 acceptance contracts, 18 schemas and both original research tracks are preserved. The original IDs remain traceable even where duplicate predevelopment, backend, final-qualification or release gates are folded into the parent. `traceability/FORECAST_PACKET_CROSSWALK.json` states the exact aliases.

### 28.1 Native ownership and model pipeline

Use Qlib's research/data/processor/recorder architecture behind VAN-owned adapters, LightGBM as the initial tabular expected-return/classification/quantile/ranking family, NeuralForecast temporal/distributional families (including N-HiTS, N-BEATSx and DeepAR) and admitted contextual challengers, plus Freqtrade/FreqAI-derived lifecycle, warmup, look-ahead, expiration and training diagnostics. These are selected capabilities, not a replacement trading application. Neither Qlib's executor nor a second Freqtrade live runtime may submit orders; VATI remains the only risk and order authority.

`TargetSpec → DatasetManifest → FeatureManifest → chronological TrainingJobSpec → candidate artifact → qualified evaluator → calibration/uncertainty record → ForecastPacket → admitted VATI consumer → observed outcome`. Every object has an exact version, source/input digests, timestamp semantics, units and permitted-use state. Candidate formats are validated before deserialization; arbitrary untrusted executable artifacts and pickle-like unsafe loads are not an admission mechanism. Forecast serving returns READY, ABSTAINED, STALE, EXPIRED, UNAVAILABLE or INVALID with reasons, not invented numerical confidence.

A single calibration boundary distinguishes calibrated model distributions, presentation confidence and prediction-market prices. Forecasts propose evidence; RiskAuthority sizes from signed constraints. Model quality, drift, uncertainty and novelty may cause abstention or admitted reduce-risk behaviour, never a grant, widened stop or unreviewed live promotion. Retraining and weekly strategy development share versioned datasets, experiment manifests and scheduler ownership so there is no duplicated hidden research loop.

### 28.2 Prediction-market intelligence and research labs

One track uses point-in-time event probabilities/expectations and uncertainty to improve VAN's existing instrument decisions. The other implements complete binary-contract valuation, payoff-consistency/arbitrage and inventory-aware market-making **research laboratories**. All are mandatory software scope, but no new live Kalshi/Polymarket order sender is added.

Source adapters record contract wording, settlement rules, venue, observed bid/ask, depth/liquidity, event mapping, source age and source-use permissions. Similar titles are not proof that contracts have identical payoffs. Two venue observations are not automatically independent evidence. Event probability is not probability that a forex trade wins. Historical feature extraction uses the information then available, not the eventual settled answer. Prediction contracts with different resolution rules, time windows or currencies cannot be normalized into a false arbitrage.

Probability-to-asset and forecast-to-position proposals preserve target uncertainty and execution-realistic costs. They flow through the existing News D1–D7/economic-admission and VATI authority contracts, never directly from a headline, probability screen or model. The research labs model non-atomic legs, inventory/hedge exposure, gaps, fees, delay and partial executions; attractive gross spread is not net realizable profit.

### 28.3 Qualification and no-procurement scope

Implement and test the entire adapter/model/lifecycle/lab capability using permitted existing data and explicit fixtures under the owner-directed no-purchase scope. A fixture is never a real provider receipt. Source-specific access or live-policy eligibility is separate from VM location; Netcup/Oracle placement grants neither provider access nor operator eligibility. Required unavailable integration remains visibly unqualified at its gate without blocking unrelated authorized development. Do not add licence procurement, subscriptions, new financial accounts or live event-contract activation by implication.

Models and studies must survive proper chronological evaluation, realistic execution/costs and prospective evidence before economic promotion. A finding that prediction-market features add no incremental value is a useful qualified result and must be retained. It does not permit omission of required research software, and no 'backend complete' record may imply profitable models or live trading rights.

### 28.4 Future native presentation

After BG-001, native model registry, calibration, candidate/champion comparison, complete experiment history, event-expectation/source-health and research-lab contexts are integrated into Trading/Intelligence/Research—not added as another global navigation system. Their full contracts live beside every existing screen. Source/age/uncertainty and pending/abstained states are more prominent than decorative scores. The same warm-light/deep-graphite tactile DNA, clear typography, quiet accents and progressive disclosure apply throughout.

## 29. Independent evidence and anti-gap acceptance

The package checker detects inconsistent scope, broken producer edges, duplicate identities, stale generated documents, tampered inputs and impossible gate ordering. Its negative tests intentionally corrupt in-memory copies and require specific error codes; they are **not** the application acceptance suite. The runtime receipt gate must additionally validate exact issuer, signature, project/source/configuration scope, test identity, freshness and revocation through the qualified DIAL authority. No field named `signature_valid` or copied PASS text is trusted by itself.

F15 is predevelopment readiness after closed preparation evidence. BG-001 is whole-backend readiness after every required service/caller/outcome has real evidence for the declared deployment target. QA/REL certify the actual signed new app and physical owner device. They remain distinct and are currently NOT_ISSUED. A design freeze, a successful documentation self-test, a historical CI result and an owner acceptance are not interchangeable.

The source repair suite must prove both directions: original counterexamples fail the intended invariant on the pinned source, and the admitted corrected source satisfies it plus retained positive behaviours. A correction invalidates dependent backtest claims; do not rebrand old economic results as newly qualified. Missing evidence, omitted requirement/action, unavailable mandatory source, false final order state, authorization bypass or silent frontend-before-backend activity blocks the appropriate gate. The final feature acceptance chain remains owner goal → source → producer → state → caller → authority → effect → independent readback → evidence → native accessibility/visual acceptance.


## 30. Integrated high-value trading intelligence and read-only crypto research

### 30.1 Current owner direction, revision and scope

The owner instruction **“Proceed according to your recommendations”**, following the comparative review of the uploaded High-Value Trading Adoptions Rev1.0, authorizes preparation and integration of the reviewed specification changes. This revision records that scope direction. It does not manufacture an owner cryptographic signature, independent F15/BG certificate, model-promotion decision or financial mandate. The current deliverable is an owner-directed integrated specification candidate; application implementation starts only through the existing admitted packet and F15 process.

The uploaded proposal is retained unchanged as provenance. Its corrected technical companion is `research/HIGH_VALUE_TRADING_INTELLIGENCE_INTEGRATED_REV1_1.md`. It has **no independent executable schedule**. The canonical specification is this master plus its machine contracts; `plan/DEVELOPMENT_PLAN.json` is the sole schedule. Source-qualified unit IDs remain stable; the generated `VAN-PKT-*` sequence is revision-qualified and its complete Rev1.11-to-Rev1.12 mapping is included. A cached old packet number must never silently resolve to a different unit.

All 205 inherited requirements—including all 90 forecasting requirements and 20 corrective contracts—remain intact. Required Qlib, LightGBM, FreqAI-inspired mechanisms, NeuralForecast models and challenger integrations, calibration, artifact handling and prediction-market laboratories are **engineering obligations**, not optional software contingent on an early profitable result. Economic admission of their outputs remains conditional on evidence. A correct integration can report `NO_QUALIFIED_MODEL`, `REJECTED` or `INCONCLUSIVE`; a missing integration cannot use those labels to claim engineering completion.

Netcup/Oracle remain the intended compute environment. This revision adds no new data-licence purchase, paid subscription, venue account registration, funded wallet, transaction signing, trading venue or capital increase. Existing permitted access may be used; code, schemas, fixtures and unrelated dependency-ready work proceed without procurement. An unavailable required provider is recorded precisely and cannot be replaced by a synthetic success or waived to open global BG-001. Hosting location, source permissions and operator/account eligibility remain separate recorded properties.

### 30.2 Source reconciliation that must precede implementation

The preceding review inventoried all 66 advertised branch heads at 63 distinct commits, with 4,634 union paths. Its supported-text pass scanned 3,680 unique UTF-8 blobs and excluded 26 size/encoding candidates. It was a complete head-tree inventory and bounded content/import scan, **not** universal semantic qualification. A current Global DIAL read refreshed the advertised heads and found no changes. Both observations are identified by exact request and artifact references in the source register; implementation must refresh them again before any source merge.

The operative source conclusions are:

* Keep PR101/`a39f241b…` resting-order, ledger, rounding and recoverable-lockout improvements, but close the already specified execution/simulator defects before using economic results for admission. A positive distant-limit control demonstrates that pending support exists; worse-than-limit fills remain a separate defect.
* Reconcile canon/PR93 shared Jev consumers, typed learning evidence and temperament changes with the PR92 and cognitive-fabric contribution sets at the **function and caller** level. Account service and coordinator had five distinct variants in the reviewed heads. Neither largest commit count nor newest branch name establishes a complete implementation.
* Reuse the shared `dial-jev` service and existing gateway/cognition clients. Existing infrastructure is not evidence that a new token-vetting question set has predictive value.
* Existing `AllocatorV1` is a deterministic quality/edge-floor extension that continues to exclude uncalibrated confidence. It is not an already implemented calibrated-model policy. Any further ranking policy needs a separately versioned contract and tests under that same owner.
* No production imports of the named forecast libraries, implementing prediction-market adapters or concrete token-security analyzers were established in the scanned source. This is a dated tracked-source observation, not proof about uncommitted work or external services.

BE-001 must merge only independently reviewed compatible changes and prove retained callers, authority checks, state migrations and negative paths. The documentation update does not merge these branches or certify them. The existing main and protected working sources remain unchanged by this pack.

### 30.3 One owner for each mechanism

The eight new logical backend capabilities extend existing services rather than mandate eight new microservices. The service catalogue identifies producer → durable state → consumer and the exact governing unit for each.

| Mechanism | Existing owner to extend | Forbidden substitute |
|---|---|---|
| Identity, quotes, source availability and state anchors | Market-data, acquisition and event/provenance services | A parallel ungoverned data lake |
| Features and targets | Shared definitions, point-in-time datasets, Feast parity and TargetSpec | Crypto-only labels that evade chronology |
| Research/training | Existing Qlib/LightGBM/NeuralForecast/Optuna workflow | A second training scheduler or registry |
| Calibration and artifact lifecycle | Existing calibration boundary and MLflow integration | A new conflicting confidence service |
| Candidate selection | Existing opportunity arbiter and versioned allocator | Agent-owned sizing or capital reservations |
| Risk and order submission | Existing RiskAuthority, account coordinator and ExecutionRouter | New wallet, exchange sender or position book |
| Judgment | Shared `dial-jev` through registered VAN consumers | Standalone Jev trading service or credential plane |
| Evaluation | Qualified simulator, experiment ledger and account-wide replay | Scaled actual-R used for different hypothetical trades |
| Owner interaction | Existing gateway/capability catalogue and post-BG native surfaces | Early dashboard or low-trust order buttons |

Broad “T2” terminology in the upload does not move every forecast into a remote slow call. Heavy training, public RPC/indexers, source extraction and remote judgments remain asynchronous outside T0. The bounded local ForecastService produces prepared results with freshness and applicability controls. T0 neither imports the research dependency stack nor synchronously calls an indexer, remote judge or artifact registry.

### 30.4 Evidence namespaces and maturity dimensions

The upload's E1–E5 publication-quality scale and the parent E0–E6 implementation-evidence scale are different namespaces. Preserve their provenance, but never merge them into an ambiguous `evidence_grade` value.

| Source classification | Meaning | What it does not prove |
|---|---|---|
| `RS1_PRIMARY_TRANSACTIONAL` | Primary or transactionally verifiable source | Correct VAN implementation |
| `RS2_PUBLISHED_REPRODUCIBLE` | Published technical result or reproducible study | VAN-specific profit |
| `RS3_VENDOR_CAPABILITY` | Vendor-supported interface/capability | Software test, deployment or alpha |
| `RS4_SELF_REPORTED` | Self-reported performance | Independent financial verification |
| `RS5_UNVERIFIED_CREATOR` | Unverified creator/video claim | Android certification or trading edge |
| `SYNTHETIC_FIXTURE` | Authored test data | Real provider observation |

`implementation_evidence_level` retains the parent's E0 OWNER_REFERENCE through E6 OWNER_VISUALLY_ACCEPTED values, plus `SPEC_ONLY` where no implementation proof exists. Legacy grade conversion requires the original source schema. Ambiguous historical rows remain unresolved. A schema, hash or registry alias by itself grants no authority.

Record engineering state, source integration/access state, economic-use qualification and live authorization separately. A defensive veto can qualify on independently measured defensive value without positive alpha, but must also account for false rejection and must never block a mandatory protective exit. Offline fixture tests do not qualify a live source, and an inaccessible source does not mean the rest of the code cannot be completed.

### 30.5 Canonical discovery identity and the complete cohort

Canonical asset identity is `(chain_namespace, network_id, canonical_token_address)`, produced by a chain-specific validated codec and linked to the raw representation. Display names and tickers are never keys. Pool and router identities are independent and chain-qualified. A token can have multiple pools; migration changes the pool relationship and liquidity history, not the token's identity.

Do not use one universal lowercase/string-normalization function. The chain adapter must validate its address encoding and decoded identity, preserve versioned normalization rules and reject malformed or unsupported identities. Tests include duplicate tickers, chain/network collisions, multiple pools and migrations.

Persist discovery **before** the survivor funnel. Every source observation has raw hash, source/cursor identity, availability time and block/slot anchor where applicable. Retain investigated, rejected, unknown, expired, delisted, unreachable and migrated candidates. Queues are bounded and source-quota-aware; negative results bind their reason, source/rule/state version and expiry. A meaningful state or rule change can invalidate a rejection before its timer expires. Restart restores the queue from durable state without duplicate jobs.

The funnel preserves D0–D6 from the donor concept: identity/discovery, inexpensive numerical checks, contract capabilities, attribution, temporal flow, optional typed context, and executable economics. Those are logical stages under existing owners, not seven new trading agents. Exactly one survivor still receives every remaining hard gate, `NO_TRADE` remains valid, strings such as `"no"` are explicitly normalized, and creator example thresholds never become VAN defaults without measured false-positive/false-negative evidence.

### 30.6 Discrete chain-security analyzers

**Solana capability:** distinguish supported SPL Token and Token-2022 observations. Inspect mint/freeze authority and applicable transfer fees, hooks, delegates and other supported extensions. Bind results to the program/parser revision, slot, source and finality policy. An unrecognized extension or missing state is UNKNOWN, not benign. Technical pool/vault/burn roles must be evidenced before they affect concentration.

**EVM capability:** bind code, proxy and implementation identity to the chain and state observed. Inspect applicable upgrade/admin powers, transfer limits/taxes, mint/blacklist and liquidity-removal controls through supported read-only evidence. A verified proxy shell does not establish the behavior of a changed implementation. Unverified code increases uncertainty; it does not establish fraud or safety.

Both analyzers produce factual `ChainSecurityFinding` records and uncertainty-bearing features. Their verdict vocabulary is `REJECT`, `INVESTIGATE`, `NO_HARD_BLOCK`; **NO_HARD_BLOCK is not SAFE_TO_BUY**. Findings state evidence coverage, unsupported checks, observation time and expiry. A finding can inform research or a separately qualified restrictive consumer, but cannot grant a route, sign a transaction or overrule risk-reducing operations.

### 30.7 Sellability and executable economics

Buy and sell checks are independent. Where supported, run read-only simulations against the stated chain/router/contract/pool state at the declared quantity, with caller assumptions, gas/fee conventions and finality recorded. Success proves only the stated simulation result at that state. It does not promise that a later sell will execute, survive changed taxes or obtain the same liquidity.

Separate three records:

1. **ExecutableQuoteFacts:** observed side, quantity, currency, executable quoted cash/depth, provider/route and state anchor, availability/expiry and fee-inclusion components. No projected exit cash or fill probability is permitted in this facts record.
2. **ExecutionEconomicsForecast:** modeled exit proceeds, execution/failure costs or fill probabilities, with explicit target, horizon, artifact/transform/calibration lineage and applicability. A probability requires its precise observable event; an amount forecast does not masquerade as a probability.
3. **NetEdgeAssessment:** a derived economic calculation referencing the exact facts and forecast versions, uncertainty and cost convention. It identifies included costs to prevent double-counting.

For quantity `q` and horizon `h`, a cash-consistent research estimate is expected executable exit proceeds minus entry cash and any costs not already included. Compare it with the strategy's preregistered materiality/uncertainty floor and independent risk eligibility. Depth, slippage, transfer costs, gas and failure costs need not scale linearly with size. A small quote cannot be extrapolated into free liquidity at a larger quantity.

The existing evaluator repairs remain upstream: limit-price semantics, effective expiry propagation, pending-fill protection, canonical order state, terminal liquidation cash, commissions/financing, intrabar ambiguity and full-account risk competition. Do not call the new economics qualified merely because a cost estimate is used to screen candidates. The simulated cash ledger must actually charge the appropriate costs. All DEX trade outcomes remain hypothetical here; there is no new signing wallet or execution client.

### 30.8 Temporal wallets, attribution and reorganization

Persist comparable anchored snapshots of holdings, supply, transfers, liquidity and role labels. Compute changes only between observations with compatible coverage. A single snapshot cannot establish growth or accumulation. A routed transfer is not automatically a genuine purchase; common funding is not conclusive common ownership.

Retain raw concentration and separately derived role-adjusted concentration with every exclusion explained. Proven technical pool/vault/router/burn accounts can be treated appropriately without deleting the raw history. Attribution is uncertainty-bearing. Heuristic clusters must retain assumptions, evidence and revision; legitimate airdrops/OTC/internal transfers must appear in negative controls.

Young assets need explicit lookback coverage. Missing preceding windows remain unknown instead of receiving a fabricated denominator. Deduplication uses the chain's transaction/log/instruction identity under the relevant adapter. Disappearing pools, source lag and unobservable exits are retained as censoring/missingness, not zero loss or an excuse to delete a cohort row.

Maintain transitive provenance from source/state → facts → features → forecasts → study/consumer. Reorganizations, corrected observations, changed authority/implementation and pool migrations produce versioned invalidation batches. Every affected cached result loses eligibility before reuse. Revalidation is idempotent and checkpointed; a crash must not leave a stale descendant ready. Historical observations and decisions remain immutable, while new analytical versions record the correction. Finality policy itself is versioned and cannot silently upgrade an observation.

### 30.9 Matched opportunities and account-level evaluation

The existing cognitive shadow book remains a **same-trade, size-only attribution view** under its declared proportional assumptions. `actual_R × multiplier` is not an independently observed hypothetical trade, a different path's own-risk R, or a faithful treatment of nonlinear costs. Preserve that useful limited view; do not stretch its claims.

Use the existing experiment and account-replay services for changed discovery, selection, timing, route or exit policy. Seal the decision opportunities, universe, feature/quote versions, both arm decisions, unresolved rows and simulator assumptions before outcomes mature. Each arm maintains its own account state, pending orders, reservations, cash, risk and later opportunity eligibility. Skipping a trade changes future opportunity cost. Summing unconstrained single-instrument simulations does not produce a feasible portfolio.

Tag hypothetical outcomes `SIMULATED_COUNTERFACTUAL`. Forward paper results and actual venue outcomes retain their own evidence classes. Paired replay estimates **incremental policy performance under its stated assumptions**; it does not identify a causal effect simply because two policies were simulated. Use prespecified event/time/asset-cluster-aware resampling, exposure-matched and simple-calendar controls, all losing trials and sequential-testing rules. Report net cash/R, coverage, abstention, false rejection, unresolved outcomes, risk, drawdown, costs and operational overhead.

The upload's proposed positive lower-95%-confidence-bound rule becomes a candidate setting within the **one** versioned validation policy, not another admission service. Materiality, sample adequacy, clustering, multiplicity and stopping rules must be locked before results. Preserve existing DSR/PBO and other applicable gates; do not overwrite them with a convenient scalar. Inadequate evidence produces INCONCLUSIVE.

### 30.10 Targets, Qlib/LightGBM and NeuralForecast integration

Use the already required model adapters rather than separate crypto-only models by default. New features and targets flow through the common point-in-time dataset, feature/target specification, Qlib study, LightGBM/NeuralForecast integration, Optuna trial record, calibration and MLflow artifact lineage.

Terminal return, after-cost strategy result, maximum adverse excursion, stop touch and fill probability are distinct targets. They differ in path dependence, horizon, label maturity and censoring. A lower terminal-return quantile cannot be relabelled a calibrated probability of hitting a protective stop. Model output probabilities, ranking scores, attribution confidence and Jev answer quality are likewise not interchangeable.

Maintain the full NeuralForecast engineering scope from §28, including direct model integration, retained temporal scenarios, contextual challengers and secure persistence. Evaluate added wallet/security/liquidity features first against the appropriate simple and tabular baselines. Economic rejection of a domain experiment does not remove the underlying model capability from the required programme.

Preserve prediction-market contract semantics, grouped calibration, asset-response models, execution replay, arbitrage and market-making research. A07's probability-context ablation is an experiment within that programme, not a replacement for it. Cross-venue agreement is not independent corroboration; shared sources across models cannot be counted as multiple independent signals.

### 30.11 Jev question sets and qualified ranking

Use the actual shared `dial-jev` consumer and existing exact evidence resolver. Registered question sets must be narrow and falsifiable—for example, whether two supplied identifiers conflict, whether a documented claim is supported, or whether the provided observations are temporally comparable. Include unknown/unanswered, question-set version, snapshot/input hash, provider/model identity, deadline, cost and expiry. No private credentials or prompt-supplied broker instructions enter the model context.

Run rule/no-LLM, Jev and any approved challenger on the same sealed case set. Measure correct/incorrect/unknown answers, false rejection, genuinely observable label calibration, cost and incremental policy value. Answer quality does not imply a profitable-trade probability. A new domain question set needs its own evaluation even when transport and existing attention/browser questions are already implemented.

The current AllocatorV1 continues unchanged as the inherited baseline. A model-informed ranking amendment must have a new explicit policy/version and qualified input contract at the **same** owner. It may select among otherwise eligible candidates; it does not size, widen risk, reserve cash independently or turn a high model score into a second mandate. Revocation restores the qualified baseline while preserving pending-order reservations.

### 30.12 Serving, recovery and determinism

Prepared forecasts/judgments carry exact state, transform, target, model, calibration, source availability and expiry. Incompatible, non-finite, stale, revoked or reorganized inputs cannot become current evidence. The positive valid path must be exercised as well as all refusal paths; a permanently unbound producer is not an implemented integration.

Keep four different reproducibility claims distinct: exact canonical replay of persisted evidence; repeatable fixed-seed inference in a pinned environment; declared cross-device numeric tolerance; and stochastic retraining reproducibility assessed statistically. No promise of byte-identical neural/GPU training follows merely from a frozen dataset. Record actual environment, configuration and tolerance rather than weakening deterministic risk-decision replay.

Outages, indexer lag, quota exhaustion, delayed results and interrupted revalidation must have bounded behavior and a truthful read model. Research unavailability cannot disable mandatory protective exits. Rolling back a model or data adapter does not resurrect revoked calibration or incompatible state. Retention and provider-use rules are enforced independently from audit immutability claims; record policy-governed deletions and reference invalidation where retention requires it.

### 30.13 Headless operations and post-BG owner surfaces

BE-044 completes the real producer/store/consumer joins, and BE-032/033 include their catalogue, isolation, capacity, observability, recovery and retention behavior. API/read-model construction is backend work. **No new native UI is authorized before the sole global BG-001**, even if one feed is already healthy.

The six new context obligations are progressive-disclosure views within the existing Trading/Research/Analyst/Work/Settings navigation, not six mandatory global pages: discovery funnel; state-bound security finding; wallet/flow evidence; sellability and economics; matched-cohort report; and typed judgment trace. Each retains native accessibility, offline/stale/unknown states, dark/light tactile design and evidence drill-down. They introduce no order, signing or funding action.

Counts reconcile total observed, rejected by reason, investigated, eligible, economically assessed, paper-simulated and actually filled **only where that evidence exists**. Research-only crypto can correctly show zero actual fills. Fixture, delayed, simulated, shadow and observed classes are visible from provenance, not from the appearance of a chart. No simplified green “safe token” badge is permitted.

### 30.14 Dependency integration and complete acceptance

New BE-037–044 are logical capabilities in the existing plan. Existing BE-001/003/010/013/021/024/032/033/035 and forecast dataset, target, calibration, serving and evaluation units receive additive contracts rather than duplicate siblings. BQ-008 verifies chain/source/finality end-to-end behavior; BQ-009 verifies full-cohort economics, evidence semantics, judgment/ranking and authority separation. Both feed BQ-006 and the sole global BG-001. All old units and their original exit obligations remain.

The added requirements and their positive, adverse and recovery contracts are machine-readable. Every requirement maps to existing/new unit owners, a producer and durable state, downstream consumer, planned evidence and eventual owner context where appropriate. All application acceptance records remain NOT_RUN in this documentation delivery; document mutations and synthetic schema checks cannot change those states.

Legacy M0–M9 and G0–G6 are explanatory crosswalk aliases. Their old ordering, reduced optional scope and M8 single-feed entry condition have no authority. The application runner must bind `(plan_revision, scope_digest, unit_id)`; old `VAN-PKT-*` numbers are resolved only through the revision-qualified crosswalk, never by positional coincidence.

### 30.15 No-loss and correction closure

Preserve all original requirement text, existing corrective contracts, 18 Product Truth atoms, 93 earlier features, 171 source atoms, 349 historical owner actions and the locked visual bytes. Original source/test documents remain lineage. Changed plan dependencies, prepared locks and source mappings are explicit revision deltas, not forged updates to signed inputs. The package includes a byte-preservation report and old-to-new schedule map.

The review's R01–R12 concerns are closed **at the specification level** by linked amendments, schemas, owner mappings and tests. The associated application defects and integrations remain open until actual implementation and appropriate receipts. Previously reproduced paper-limit/expiry counterexamples are retained as evidence and regression requirements; this revision does not claim to have fixed their code.

### 30.16 Implementation entry and exclusions

The next executable programme remains PRE-001 source/feature reconciliation, PRE-002 independent semantic/contract review, then PRE-003 F15 with the required exact owner trust binding. Updating the document and passing its checks is not a substitute for those exits. Once admitted, dependency-ready backend source consolidation and implementation proceeds on the authorized DIAL VM topology; no physical desktop or silent alternative runtime is part of the plan.

Existing Zuul admission and typed Global DIAL deployment remain the routes. No new GitHub Actions deployment authority, hosted tunnel, IAM, firewall, credential, paid account or broad infrastructure repair is included. Release and model/strategy live admission remain separate. The desired result is a fully implemented, honestly evaluated capability—not an obligation to claim profitable forecasting or fabricate unavailable integration evidence.


## 31. Rev 1.13 source-state review and canonical reconciliation

**Owner-requested revision, prepared 18 October 2026.** This chapter is normative over overlapping Rev1.12 documentary statements. Original Rev1.12 requirements, 131 unique unit IDs, 237 requirement IDs, 234 contextual surfaces, 1,287 original test contracts, source/REA references and feature coverage remain inherited. **No new app code, trading mandate, model, funded venue, operational deployment or F15/BG certificate is issued.**

### 31.1 Source freshness and branch disposition

Live read-only comparison observed 66 advertised VAN branches at 63 distinct head commits on 18 October. `main=8eb1889822fc8a83f219977c15340172e16acd91`; PR101/Opus=`a39f241b0135cf92cc5a6b9a28af226aa294603d`, not merged. The source review must consider every candidate branch; a focused matrix alone is not a full 66-row admission. See `consolidation/REV113_LIVE_REPOSITORY_BRANCH_REVIEW.json` and `consolidation/REV113_REPOSITORY_RECONCILIATION.md` for exact source SHA, PR98/99 preservation and review limits.

All 237 requirements, 234 screen contexts and 349 historical owner actions retain their current identity. Every `OWNER_RETIRE` requires a separate signed decision. The Rev1.13 material contract is **not** a workaround to import old Compose panels or replace a functional service with a beautiful illustration.

### 31.2 Honest evidence

Rev1.12 passed documentary, schema and exported DAG verification on its recorded source. It did **not** pass its 1,287 application tests; all remained `NOT_RUN`. Rev1.13 adds further **planned** test obligations, still `NOT_RUN`. Neither the 66-head index nor line-level hashes prove all candidate function semantics, runtime registration, signed market actions or production applicability. Predevelopment must close their actual causal feature map before an independent F15 can certify build readiness.

### 31.3 The only development sequence

`PRE-001 → PRE-002 → independently issued PRE-003/F15 → 82 backend capability units → 12 independent backend-verification units → BG-001 → UX-001/UX-002/UX-003 → FE-001…FE-023 → QA-001…QA-005 → signed rollout → proven legacy retirement.`

This is a *partial order*, not a claim that every unit has to run serially or that 82 services must be separately deployed. Parallel backend work requires dependency exit, resource governor placement, pinned source contract and source-authenticated receipts. All headless service contract integration happens **before** any new frontend UI renderer is built. Old five UI early flows remain future integration-test cases only; they do not authorise a frontend bypass or define the product scope.

## 32. Deterministic trading integrity and research-quality backend

### 32.1 One authority chain, seven distinct domains

Sealed owner/manual/automatic/news-intent candidates enter one Risk Authority → reservations → venue route → broker result → protection verification → position-family and ledger reconciliation → owner readback pipeline. Chart, Rive/orb, JEV, Muse, News prose, prediction markets and wallet analysis are explicitly untrusted *evidence or display* at that boundary. No alternate risk or order path can be made by a design component.

1. **Market facts:** point-in-time bars, bid and ask, instrument canonical ID, venue tick and lot constraints, observed time and source revision; `MISSING` is not interpolated into success.
2. **Proposals:** strategy/manual/news proposals explicitly typed with market/limit/stop/stop-limit/pending semantics, expiration, recipient venue and ordering metadata.
3. **Authorisation:** exact quantised executable proposal *before* risk sign-off; versioned mandate, account scope, persistent hard risk across manual and autonomous origins; no default-only risk escalation.
4. **Execution:** broker adapter enforces limit `buy_fill ≤ buy_limit`, `sell_fill ≥ sell_limit` (proper sided data), stop trigger and eventual fill separately, partial fills, price drift, no tick/PIP rounding that widens a stop.
5. **Pending lifetime:** effective policy expiry and event/blackout action propagated through router→venue; fills may race cancellation; always reconcile actual broker exposures.
6. **Protection:** immediate and pending fill share one proof/readback path. Entry stop included in same broker request where qualified, but order submission does not imply stop **verified**. Missing protection produces the declared containment/flatten-and-halt policy. No concealed unprotected position.
7. **Ledger and owner projection:** integrity-checked append-only event log with durable hash checkpoints and bounded verification; state reduction includes PLACED/PARTIAL/MODIFY_PENDING/CANCEL_PENDING/EXPIRED/CANCELLED/OUTCOME_UNKNOWN and never visually confuses a cancellation request with confirmed cancellation.

Any unexpected fill, unknown funds transfer, missing stop, model failure, stale source, locked account, lost ledger checkpoint or unqualified provider must produce a **named refusal or unknown** and independently observable attention; never optimistic GREEN.

### 32.2 Account/funds and persistent risk semantics

Local account creation/edit/unlink is distinct from broker registration and remote closure. Demo/read-only/investor scopes cannot silently acquire funding or trading authority. Funds quotes bind fee, FX, beneficiary, amount, identity, expiry, provider and owner signature. Submission may return OUTCOME_UNKNOWN; no blind retries; provider settlement readback is required for `SETTLED/PAID`. Position margin and pending reservations are checked before withdrawal. Funding cash flows never become trading P&L; performance and drawdown use flow-adjusted equity with exact currency units. Policy-driven 24-hour cooldown, half-size probation and escalation after more than two cooldowns within seven days follow the accepted OD-39 values **only through the approved signed runtime mandate**. A restart may not reset it. Manual/autonomous cooldown scope is explicit; total-account hard risk still applies to both.

### 32.3 Qualified evaluation before strategy-development automation

An automated weekly strategy loop must depend on qualified evaluator BE-035. Simulator uses chronological bid/ask and venue-specific order matching: pending orders do not fill at unreachable prices; limit orders never execute worse than their limit; ambiguous within-bar ordering is resolved by suitable intrabar data or pessimistic/interval outcomes, not arbitrary OHLC sequencing. Original arrival timestamps, market calendars, spreads, slippage, commissions, constraints, rejections and delay distributions are preserved in immutable dataset/cost manifests. A future-only perturbation must leave prior decisions bit-equivalent and a cheating strategy must fail the look-ahead sentinel. A yearly grouping is *not* walk-forward; folds require real before/after data, a frozen selection policy and sealed holdouts. DSR/PBO are never labelled a chance of true talent or guaranteed profitability.

BE-036's weekly condition loop examines both winners and losers, evaluates all attempted candidates under the same cost and sampling policy, retains parent lineage and failed trials, and can legitimately return `NO_QUALIFIED_CANDIDATE`. Live promotion is **never** a side effect of the research run: independent evidence, owner signature, original Risk Authority and exact service binding are required. A model with high accuracy and negative net-of-cost economics remains in research.

### 32.4 Full ML/forecast/crypto research obligations preserved

Qlib, LightGBM, NeuralForecast challengers, model calibration, feature parity, prediction-market read-only intelligence, token discovery/finality/DEX economics and shared JEV remain admitted software-development obligations in the existing 82 backend and 12 BQ units. Their outputs are separately evidence-gated for economic use. Unknown licence/source availability is a *concrete provisioning gate*, not a reason to declare an unimplemented adapter qualified. Read-only crypto research does not create a token-trading or wallet-signing authority. Deterministic source/feature/label boundaries and safe abstention remain mandatory regardless of market hypothesis profitability.

## 33. Backend quality: production-candidate polish, not merely API coverage

A backend capability is complete only when its domain code, source identity, typed request/responses, versioned state, migration, governance, runtime caller, failure/restart recovery, telemetry, negative tests, independent receipt, bounded resource use and documented runbook all match. One screenshot, import, class, unit test, schema or provider HTTP 200 does not qualify it. The backend may use shared services rather than inventing 82 separate daemons; each logical unit still has a named owner and proof.

### 33.1 Service architecture principles

- **Vertical contracts inside backend, no duplicate truth:** VATI alone owns risk and order effects; broker/providers own monetary settlement; DIAL owner gateway owns exact signed actions; Hermes/Muse own task/provider execution; shared Jev supplies advisory evidence. Android is a consumer and navigation/proposal surface, not another backend.
- **Modular topology:** typed adapters isolate gateway, trading, ML, research, graph, browser, providers and observability; asynchronous events contain project/account scope, source ID/revision, observed and effective time, causal/trace IDs and expiry. Race, retries, dedupe and replay are explicitly modelled.
- **Reliability:** graceful bounded drain, outbox with idempotent effect identity, queue/backpressure and circuit breakers; DNS/credential rotation and storage migration have documented rollback. Production monitoring distinguishes failed, rejected, delayed, unknown and stale.
- **Security/privacy:** mTLS and real provider ownership gates, least-privilege typed actions, encryption and provenance, immutable audit, redacted logs, consent and retention. Root/VM grants never bypass user-facing financial or device authority.
- **Performance:** repeated source integrity checking uses a verified checkpoint, not a full history scan per decision; ticks and feed ingestion remain bounded; memory/cache eviction is measured and tested under VM pressure. GPU models and background research never block the T0 event/execution path.
- **Research:** all models use versioned datasets, features, labels and trial ledgers; training is cost/resource bounded; model and forecasting fallbacks abstain rather than invent a profitable trade.

### 33.2 BG-001: complete backend means complete backend

`BG-001` refuses any of the 82 required backend units or 12 independent verifier units without full scoped evidence, any unhandled approved owner action, an unmaterialised required provider, an unqualified instrument/order type, missing private-key safety, a blocked critical source, or a stale/unsigned certificate. Tests that require a real provider must run in its admitted sandbox or named live read-only setting—not be replaced with synthetic success. Unsupported provider capabilities are accurately refused at runtime and must be distinguishable from an unimplemented **required** integration.

The shared backend publishes a fully source-bound **234-context consumer catalogue**: canonical route, operations, permissions, state/freshness, error/recovery/unknown modes, source identity and receipt links. UX-001 begins only when this contract is complete. Backend changes after BG invalidate affected consumer/visual contracts until qualified; no hidden trading logic should migrate to the Android screen to avoid reopening a gate.

## 34. Owner visual authority: Graphite/Porcelain Material DNA Rev 2

The accepted dark and light images remain locked. The owner's Graphite Command style document is strong at identifying physical dark theme roles (115 tokens, 16 recipes, five specimens) but insufficient alone to assert dual-theme, all-app identity.
