Rev 2 extends it into **paired material roles**, 30 domain-specific recipes and mappings for all 234 contexts—see `design/VAN_TACTILE_SPATIAL_STYLE_REV2.md`, `design/VAN_MATERIAL_TOKENS_REV2.json` and `design/ALL_234_SURFACE_MATERIAL_ACCEPTANCE_REV1_13.md`.

**Preserve:** sculpted metallic Home orb, believable contact shadows, satin/brushed graphite material, warm silver porcelain alternative, quiet section hierarchy, precision chart plot and calm restrained cyan accents. **Improve:** native adaptive Android insets, layered role-specific material elevations, state-aware meaningful orb motion, all screen families, source/financial truth, accessibility and performance budgets. **Reject:** flat stock Material rectangles, glass/neon saturation, mini dashboards everywhere, iPhone Dynamic Island in Android, arbitrarily glowing profit cards, Home state legend, fake financial amounts, unsigned direct chart actions or a fully dense Home sheet.

Home Orb and Floating VAN share one typed reducer but remain independent UI surfaces. The large orb tap opens a tiny navigation/notification sheet, not another information-dense dashboard. Trading Home exposes Markets, Watchlist, Potential trades, Chart and Intelligence; deep full chart, accounts, risk, News and work remain accessible through contextual presentation. A line chart is a data plane, not a decorative surface; the precision plot stays relatively flat while physical controls remain dimensional. A News report includes sources/history/opposition and current impacts but starts with short facts, not a wall of text. Project progress diagrams show only verified nodes and agent edges and offer a fully accessible list.

Every one of the 234 contexts has assigned material-family, focal recipe, detail recipe and both-theme/state acceptance. The style's **canonical use is after BG-001**, with owner-approved native goldens on an actual Android build. Tokens are provisional during specification and must not be marked owner-approved until dark/light device goldens are accepted.

## 35. Integrated all-branch preservation and full-surface design

The source baseline remains unmerged `PR101 and main`; no automatic cherry-pick is allowed. PRE-001 must reconcile all 66 heads (including PR98 native owner controls and PR99 cinematic Studio) at file/function/caller level; PRE-002 maps them into Product Truth and owner action ledger; independent F15 tests source completeness; BE-001 admits compatible source code. No earlier branch with a richer service is overridden merely because its date is older, and no late branch with a different owner authority supersedes the verified canon by default.

B1 is full-product completion, not five selected journeys; B4 is engineering-owned gap closure, not owner acceptance of incompleteness. The 237 requirements and 234 contexts do not silently replace the 78 historical surfaces or 349 source owner-action obligations; they reference and reconcile them. All target domain features—Trading, Accounts/Funds, prediction research, News, Work/Agents, Memory, Automation, Floating VAN, voice, browser, ARTEMIS, Settings, Connected, Studio and device controls—are mandatory when admitted, regardless of whether they appeared in an initial poster.

## 36. Revised deterministic development plan and tests

The authoritative machine graph remains **131 stable packets** with all **82 backend and 12 backend-verification** units before **BG-001/VAN-PKT-098**, then UX-001/002/003 and 23 frontend units, then QA/Release/Retirement. This revision refines *exit contracts, evidence and visual binding*, not ordering, scopes or unit identity. It adds 41 proposed application acceptance contracts; original 1,287 tests are preserved unchanged and every new test is `NOT_RUN`. The rendered plan is mechanically regenerated as `plan/VAN_NEXT_DETERMINISTIC_PLAN_REV1_13.md`, with updated provenance. Source changes or actual owner amendments must invalidate affected checks; do not self-issue an F15, BG or production success state.

**Documentary verification is only a schema, graph, mapping and deterministic-output check**. Passing these does not demonstrate signed Android delivery, broker settlement, profitable strategy, qualified inference, independent source semantic acceptance or device quality.

## 37. Readiness, hard gates and decision ownership

Current status: `SPEC_RECONCILED_PENDING_OWNER_AND_INDEPENDENT_ADMISSION`.

1. Owner Product Truth, original visual locks and specific financial/trading decisions remain owner-authorised; this pack does not sign for the owner.
2. PRE-001/002 source/function/action crosswalk and semantic audit remain required before independent PRE-003/F15. A README saying "fully reviewed" is not a certificate.
3. Actual operation proof, funding provider sandboxes, market data rights, native device and retraining cost/resource measurements must close at their specified stage. Missing required rails or unjustified workarounds block the applicable stage; no owner acceptance of an unfinished component.
4. The signer/authority of F15/BG/physical qualifications must be independent and current; certificate validity includes source/config/contract/revocation digests.
5. No code merge, new OCI/VPN permission, payment, deposit, withdrawal, production order, model promotion, signed mandate or release is authorised solely by this specification.

**The resulting product must be one coherent VAN:** deterministic financially safe backend, governed opportunity intelligence and strategy research, professional chart and Accounts/Funds, full agent/project command experiences and an exceptionally calm two-theme Tactile Spatial Android UI applied role by role to every accepted surface. Completing the app requires independent observed outcomes, not just completion of this document.
